Document 002: 10年期美债收益率创24年新高，Amundi、Pimco逆势加仓：实际回报近3%

Source PDF: all report pdf/10年期美债收益率创24年新高，Amundi、Pimco逆势加仓：实际回报近3%.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

美债剧烈抛售推⾼ 10 年期收益率⾄ 5.36% 的 24 年⾼位，但⾼⾼达 3% 的实际回报率触发了顶
级机构的逆势布局。 Amundi 与 Pimco 等资产管理巨头正趁估值回调及股债⽐价逆转之际加
⼤美债配置，三季度美债基⾦净流⼊创新⾼。
摘要：
美债遭遇持续抛售、 10 年期收益率触及 24 年峰值之际，全球顶级资产管理机构正反向布局，将
这轮史罕⻅的⾼收益率视为锁定⻓期固定回报的历史性窗⼝。
Amundi ⾸席投资官 Vincent Mortier 表⽰，这家管理规模达 2.6 万亿欧元的资产管理巨头正在 " 逐
步增加 " 美元计价投资组合对美债的敞⼝。Pimco ⾸席投资官 Dan Ivascyn 则指出，尽管美债价格
可能继续下跌，但与处于历史⾼位附近的股票相⽐，美债估值更具吸引⼒。市场数据显⽰，以
通胀预期折算， 10 年期美债⽬前提供的实际回报率接近 3% ，这⼀⽔平正在吸引⼤量机构资⾦⼊
场。
资⾦流向数据印证了上述机构的⾏动。据彭博根据 EPFR 数据的测算，今年第三季度美国政府债

券基⾦净流⼊资⾦达到创纪录的 593 亿美元， 10 ⽉以来更已追加流⼊近 140 亿美元。
收益率冲顶，触发逢⾼买⼊
美债市场今年以来经历了⼀轮罕⻅的剧烈抛售。 10 年期美债收益率年内累计上⾏逾 106 个基点，
上周三在拍卖前数⼩时攀升⾄ 5.36% ，创下 24 年来新⾼。此轮抛售由多重因素驱动，包括能源
成本因特朗普政府对伊朗战争政策⽽上升、⼈⼯智能公司⼤规模发债以及市场对美国经济持续
强劲增⻓的预期。
然⽽，正是这⼀⾼收益率⽔平触发了机构的逆势布局。 Mortier 指出，市场对未来⼗年平均通胀
率约 2.35% 的预期，意味着当前 10 年期美债可提供接近 3% 的实际回报， " 这⼀⽔平⾃然会吸引新
的兴趣 " 。
从拍卖结果来看，机构需求已现端倪。上周三 390 亿美元 10 年期国债拍卖需求强劲，⼀级交易商
—— 即负责吸纳未被投资者认购部分的⼤型银⾏ —— 认购⽐例降⾄ 2003 年以来最低，显⽰市场
投资者直接认购意愿显著增强。上周的 3 年期和 30 年期国债拍卖同样表现稳健。

相对价值凸显，股债⽐较优势逆转
机构加仓美债的逻辑，不仅来⾃绝对收益的吸引⼒，更源于股债相对价值的重新评估。
Pimco 的 Dan Ivascyn 表⽰，当前股票估值⾼度拉伸、信⽤利差极度收窄，企业债相对于超低⻛
险的美国国债所提供的额外补偿极为有限， " 当你审视股票估值有多紧绷、信⽤利差有多压缩，
我们认为这些利率⽔平更具吸引⼒。 "
PGIM Credit 联席⾸席投资官 Greg Peters 态度同样明确： " 美国 10 年期收益率站上 5% ，是⼀个不
错的中⻓期持有标的，是相当好的投资。 "
Morgan Stanley Investment Management 宽市场固定收益主管 Vishal Khanduja 则认为，当前收
益率⽔平 " 应当⾜够吸引投资者将资⾦从现⾦、浮动利率证券或其他配置中转移出来，锁定固定
利率回报 " 。

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

期限选择分歧，⻓端⻛险仍受争议
尽管对美债整体前景看法趋于积极，机构投资者在期限选择上仍存在明显分歧，⻓端美债⾯临
的供需技术⾯压⼒令部分投资者保持谨慎。
Peters 直⾔： " 我不确定我会配置 30 年期美债。 "Khanduja 将 10 年期以内品种标记为 " 有吸引⼒ " ，
但表⽰对 20 年和 30 年期的 " 确信度较低 " 。他指出，期限越⻓，供需技术⾯的⼲扰越剧烈，并特别
提及财政部⻓⻉森特扩⼤美国国债回购规模的决定 ——" 当⾮经济性参与者介⼊并扭曲该期限段
的供需技术⾯时，很难建⽴清晰的估值逻辑。 "
对于急于 " 抄底 " 但可能买在半⼭腰的⻛险， Khanduja 以数学逻辑作出回应：
展望更⼴泛的资⾦流向， Peters 还指出欧元区局势可能为美债带来额外的资⾦流⼊。法国主权债

务在明年春季⼤选临近之际遭遇⼤幅抛售。 " 我完全可以预⻅，⼀旦法国出现问题，欧元区收益
率进⼀步联动攀升，资⾦将更多地流⼊美国国债市场。 " 他说。
" 收益所提供的缓冲已经⾜够充裕，在这些价位持有⽐等待收益率的终极顶部或底部更为理性。 "

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國服務業與內需維持擴張，通膨黏著與中東風險牽動政策前景 
經濟 
⚫ 9 月美國 ISM 服務業 PMI 由 55.4 降至 54.9，連續第 27 個月維持擴張。
商業活動及新訂單雖較前月回落 ， 仍保持穩健成長 ； 就業重返擴張區間 ，
內需與勞動市場仍具韌性 。 惟價格指數續升創高 ， 出口與進口動能轉弱 ，
後續仍須關注通膨壓力及外需變化。 
⚫ 8 月美國商品及服務貿易逆差擴大至 1,056 億美元 ， 較上月增加13.7%。
出口 3,152 億美元、進口4,208 億美元，分別月增1.4%及 4.3%。出口成
長主要受非貨幣黃金、原油及半導體帶動，進口則以原油、黃金及資本
財增加為主 。 累計前8 月貿易逆差 5,570 億美元 ， 較去年同期縮減19.9%，
惟近期進口增幅高於出口，使逆差再度擴大。 
⚫ 9 月 FOMC 會議紀要顯示，AI 投資持續帶動經濟與就業動能，勞動市
場仍具韌性；惟去通膨進展有限，核心服務通膨及成本轉嫁壓力仍存。
全體官員支持升息 1 碼，傾向維持限制性政策，並關注 AI 融資、公債
市場波動及網路安全等金融風險。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期美伊緊張情勢再升溫，川普改口不排除選前對伊朗採取軍事行動，
伊朗則持續尋求重啟談判；同時葉門青年運動頻繁襲擊沙國目標，加劇
中東安全風險。若衝突擴大，恐影響荷姆茲與曼德海峽能源運輸，推升
油氣價格並增添全球經濟下行風險。 
⚫ 美國啟動 337 調查，針對 AI 伺服器垂直供電系統（VPD）相關專利侵
權爭議展開審查，鴻海、台達電、廣達等約 20 家業者列名。由於涉及
AI 伺服器關鍵供電技術，若最終認定侵權，相關產品恐面臨輸美限制，
對供應鏈營運帶來影響。 
短評 
⚫ 美國服務業與內需仍具韌性，AI 投資亦支撐經濟動能，惟貿易逆差擴
大、成本壓力升高及通膨黏著，使聯準會仍需維持偏緊縮立場。加上美
伊與中東局勢升溫，能源價格與金融市場波動風險上升，後續景氣不確
定性仍高。

重要 
事件 
⚫ 西班牙政府宣布於 11 月 29 日提前進行大選。 
⚫ 法國 10 年期公債殖利率高出德國 130 個基點以上，主權風險升溫。 
⚫ 歐中談判最終達成 16 項共識，依據歐盟發布聲明，中國將自主減少混
合動力車對歐盟的出口量 5 成以上 ， 雙方同意降低部分產品MFN 稅率 ，
預期 2027 年 3 月雙方將再度舉行貿易會談。 
短評 
⚫ 考量法國財政不確定性較高，短期內德國與法國殖利率差距恐難縮小。 
⚫ 法國製造業在出口需求疲弱及財政支撐相對有限下 ， 景氣復甦力道恐仍
較為溫和，預料德、法兩國經濟及工業活動表現分化的情形短期內仍將
持續。 
日 
本 
薪資成長支撐內需，消費信心持穩 
經濟 ⚫ 8 月薪資年增 3.8%。 ⚫ 9 月消費者信心下降 0.1 點至 35.4。 
短評 ⚫ 雖然通膨預期回升與物價壓力抑制部分消費信心 ， 但薪資與所得成長仍持續支撐家庭支出，使消費者信心維持溫和復甦趨勢。 
中 
國 
歐中達成貿易談判，外部風險暫時降溫 
經濟 ⚫ 中國 9 月外匯存底較 8 月減少 381 億美元至 3.4 兆美元。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 中國財政部宣布動用 5,500 億元人民幣的地方政府債務額度，資金將優
先用於「六網」基礎建設等重點項目，加強穩增長措施。 
短評 
⚫ 歐中談判最終達成 16 項共識，依據歐盟發布聲明，中國將自主減少混
合動力車對歐盟的出口量 5 成以上 ， 雙方同意降低部分產品MFN 稅率 ，
預期 2027 年 3 月雙方將再度舉行貿易會談。

世銀上調經濟展望；印度央行近四年來首度升息 
經濟 
⚫ 世銀上調東亞及太平洋地區 2026 年 GDP 預估至 4.5%；印度 2026 財年
GDP 預估上調至 7.1%。 
⚫ 越南 2026 年第三季 GDP 年增率達 9.95%。 
重要 
事件 ⚫ 印度央行升息一碼至 5.5%，以應對通膨及匯率壓力。 
短評 
⚫ 世銀認為人工智慧 （AI） 順風仍足以抵銷能源風險，但警告亞洲地區經
濟高度依賴 AI 相關製造業和出口，使其容易受到全球科技支出逆轉的
影響，此外，能源震撼延續到明年，補貼政策恐難以為繼。 
台 
灣 
出口動能強勁，物價壓力與貿易風險並存 
經濟 ⚫ 9 月消費者物價指數年增 2.73%。 ⚫ 9 月出口額為 872.2 億美元。 
重要
事件 
⚫ 美國國際貿易委員會 （USITC)以 337 條款 ， 對特定垂直供電系統及相關
產品啟動專利侵權調查，其中台達電、廣達、鴻海及旗下相關企業等遭
列名，後續應關注調查結果及對台灣電源供應與伺服器供應鏈的影響。 
短評 
⚫ 9 月出口續創佳績，反映 AI 相關需求及科技產品拉貨動能強勁，外需
表現維持熱絡；9 月 CPI 創近兩年半新高，主要受天候因素及能源價格
影響，但主計總處預期 10 月將回落。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.10.12 
一、 美國—美國服務業與內需維持擴張，通膨黏著與中東風險牽動政策前景 
2026 年 9 月美國 ISM 服務業採購經理人指數(Services PMI) 為 54.9，較上月
55.4 下降 0.5 個百分點，已連續第 27 個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，且高於近 12
個月平均值 54.1，服務業景氣仍持續穩健擴張。細項觀察，商業活動指數由 61.7
降至 56.5，新訂單指數由 60.9 降至 59.8，雖較前月回落，但仍維持高檔擴張；就
業指數則由 47.8 回升至 50.1，三個月來首度重返擴張區間。價格指數由 72.6 升
至 74.0，創 2022 年 7 月以來新高。供應商交貨指數由 51.3 升至 53.2，交貨時間
再度延長；庫存指數由56.7 升至 57.8，企業持續提前備貨以因應未來成本上升風
險。此外，未完成訂單指數升至 56.6，創 2022 年 7 月以來高點，惟新出口訂單
指數由 56.3 大幅降至 46.9，八個月來首度跌入收縮區間，進口指數亦由 56.3 降
至 52.9，外部需求及採購動能均降溫。整體而言，美國服務業景氣仍維持穩健擴
張，內需與就業展現一定韌性，但燃料價格上漲、關稅政策、供應鏈限制及地緣
政治等因素持續推升企業成本，後續仍須關注通膨壓力及外需變化對景氣的影響。 
2026 年 8 月美國商品及服務貿易逆差擴大至 1,056 億美元 ， 較上月修正後928
億美元增加 127 億美元，增幅 13.7%。當月出口 3,152 億美元，較上月增加 45 億
美元(+1.4%)；進口 4,208 億美元，較上月增加 172 億美元(+4.3%)。其中，商品貿
易逆差擴大至 1,366 億美元，服務貿易順差則小幅增至 310 億美元。出口成長主
要受工業用品及原材料出口增加 63 億美元帶動，其中非貨幣黃金、原油及燃油
出口增幅較為顯著，資本財出口亦受半導體、電腦及電腦周邊設備需求增加所支
撐；進口方面，工業用品及原材料進口增加 91 億美元，資本財進口增加 62 億美
元，其中原油、非貨幣黃金、半導體及工業機械進口增幅較大。累計2026 年 1 至
8 月，美國商品及服務貿易逆差為 5,570 億美元，較 2025 年同期減少 1,382 億美
元，縮減 19.9%；同期出口成長 11.8%，進口增加 4.4%，顯示美國對外貿易失衡
情況較去年同期有所改善，惟近期進口增幅明顯高於出口，使貿易逆差再度呈現
擴大趨勢。 
2026 年 9 月 FOMC 會議紀要重點如下：(1)經濟：若干官員認為美國經濟成
長動能有所增強 ，AI 投資規模與速度持續超乎預期 ， 帶動企業投資與生產力提升 ；

5

企業活動則受到企業獲利維持高檔及部分政策因素支持；(2)就業：多數官員表示
勞動市場近期略有改善，就業成長維持穩健，失業率仍處低檔，整體勞動市場大
致符合充分就業狀態，供需風險大致平衡；(3)通膨：官員普遍認為近期去通膨進
展有限，非住房核心服務通膨仍偏高，人力、能源及部分進口成本轉嫁情形仍值
得關注；部分官員擔憂 AI 投資與相關支出擴張，可能推升需求並增加通膨持續
性風險；(4)貨幣政策：鑑於經濟活動持續擴張、勞動市場具韌性且通膨仍高於目
標，全體官員支持升息 1 碼。許多官員認為維持較具限制性的政策立場有助降低
通膨風險，部分官員則認為未來可能仍需進一步緊縮政策，以確保通膨回到目標
水準；(5)金融風險：官員普遍認為金融條件整體仍支持經濟成長，但部分官員關
切 AI 基礎設施快速擴張所衍生的融資風險，以及美國公債市場波動、網路安全
與其他潛在金融穩定風險。 
近期美伊及中東局勢再度升溫，市場對區域衝突擴大的疑慮升高。美國總統
川普由先前宣稱期中選舉前不會攻擊伊朗 ， 改口表示 「仍有可能」 採取軍事行動 ，
且美軍已就相關軍事選項進行準備，使美伊對抗風險再度攀升；另一方面，伊朗
雖有意透過斡旋重啟談判，但雙方在核計畫與制裁議題上仍存重大分歧。此外，
葉門青年運動持續升高對沙烏地阿拉伯攻擊，利雅德哈立德國王國際機場一週內
多次遇襲，造成多人死傷，凸顯區域安全風險升高。整體而言，若美伊衝突或代
理人戰事進一步升級，恐衝擊荷姆茲海峽及曼德海峽等能源運輸要道，推升國際
油氣價格、加劇通膨壓力，並增添全球金融市場波動及經濟成長下行風險。 
美國國際貿易委員會(USITC)於 10 月 9 日正式啟動 337 條款調查，針對特定
垂直供電系統(Vertical Power Delivery，VPD)、 相關零組件及採用該技術的運算設
備是否涉及專利侵權展開審查。此案由美國電源技術廠商 Vicor Corporation 提出
申訴，主張相關產品侵犯其專利權，並要求發布有限排除令及停止侵權命令，以
限制涉案產品進入美國市場 。 調查對象涵蓋全球約20 家企業 ， 包括台灣的鴻海 、
台達電、廣達及其美國關係企業，另有中國的立訊精密、工業富聯、德國的英飛
凌等國際大廠列名 。 本案聚焦 AI 伺服器及高效能運算領域關鍵的供電架構技術 ，
該技術有助提升晶片供電效率、降低能源損耗，已廣泛應用於資料中心與 AI 運
算設備。隨著全球 AI 基礎建設投資持續擴大，相關專利布局與授權爭議亦日益
升溫。USITC 指出，此次立案僅代表調查程序正式展開，並不表示已認定侵權，
後續將由行政法官進行聽證及審理，預計於 45 天內訂定調查時程。台灣經濟部

則表示，337 調查屬企業間智慧財產權爭議，政府將持續關注案情發展，並於必
要時提供業者相關協助 ； 若最終認定違反337 條款 ， 涉案產品可能面臨輸美限制 ，
對 AI 伺服器供應鏈帶來潛在影響。 
二、歐元區—法國殖利率短期內恐難降溫 
 德國工業生產在 7 月月減 1.2%後，8 月回彈 2%，主要反映部分汽車業者在 7
月提前進行歲修 ， 並且在8 月陸續復工後 ， 帶動生產及庫存回補需求所致 ； 此外 ，
受到 8 月萊茵河水位降低，沿岸化學工廠物流運輸受阻，致化工產業產出月減
1.2%為主要拖累因素。儘管工業生產如期反彈，惟工業訂單卻大幅下跌 10.6%，
主要是與軍工產業相關的訂單如飛機、船舶、軍用車輛、武器彈藥等訂單減少，
顯示政府前期大額採購效應逐漸消退；除軍工產業外，其餘產業訂單則大致維持
穩定。 
 法國 8 月工業生產月減 0.3%，為連續第二個月下滑，主要係夏季用電高峰過
後電力產出回落，帶動能源部門生產減少所致。另方面，製造業生產月增0.3%，
其中造紙、化工及電子電機設備等產業自前期低點回升，部分抵銷汽車等運輸設
備產出月減 2.2%的拖累。不過，法國 9 月 INSEE 新訂單指標由 8 月的-13.8 點降
至-15.8 點，顯示企業接單偏弱，主要反映出口需求疲軟。此外，相較於德國近期
透過國防及基礎建設支出擴大支撐工業生產，法國財政擴張力道相對有限，使製
造業景氣較依賴外部需求，德、法兩國製造業表現因而呈現逐漸分化的趨勢。 
 展望未來，近期歐元區各國殖利率上升，除反映通膨壓力增強、升息預增加
之外，亦部分反映西班牙宣布於 11 月 29 日提前進行大選、法國財政前景不明朗
等政治因素影響。儘管較高的殖利率有助於促使法國政府推動財政整頓，但考量
相關改革涉及福利及退休制度調整等政治敏感議題，不確定性較高，短期內德國
與法國殖利率差距恐難縮小。整體而言，法國製造業在出口需求疲弱及財政支撐
相對有限下，景氣復甦力道恐仍較為溫和，預料德、法兩國經濟及工業活動表現
分化的情形短期內仍將持續。 
三、日本—薪資成長支撐內需，消費信心持穩 
 8 月薪資年增 3.8%，其中經常性薪資年增 3.8%、加班費年增 5.2%、實質薪資
年增 1.5%， 主要受惠於春鬥加薪效應延續及通膨壓力較去年趨緩所帶動 ， 有助於
進一步提升民眾購買力，支撐消費與經濟復甦。

9 月消費者信心指數小幅下滑 0.1 點至 35.4，但表現仍優於市場預期。受食品
及商品價格持續上漲影響 ， 民眾對未來一年通膨的預期升溫 ， 預期通膨率高於2%
的家庭比例升至 79.3%，並使生活水準及耐久財購買意願略為轉弱。不過，穩健
的薪資成長及股市上漲帶來的金融收入，持續支撐家庭所得，有助於緩衝物價上
漲對消費的負面影響，顯示日本內需仍具一定韌性。 
四、中國—歐中達成貿易談判，外部風險暫時降溫 
中國 9 月外匯存底降至 3.40 兆美元，較前月減少 381 億美元，反映美元升
值、政府以外匯購買黃金儲備，加上歐美殖利率上升，造成中國持有的海外債券
資產出現評價損失，皆為外匯存底下滑的主要原因。惟亦不排除中國外匯流入情
況可能有所轉弱，例如出口成長放緩、進口需求增加，或資本外流規模高於預期
等因素。 
展望未來 ， 近期歐中談判達成16 項共識 ， 未來中國混合動力車出口至歐盟將
面臨調整壓力，但歐盟進一步升高貿易保護措施的風險已降溫。另一方面，基於
中國維持大額經常帳順差、出口商結匯需求及年底季節性資金流入支撐，預期人
民幣仍有升值空間，並帶動外匯存底回升。 
五、印度及東南亞—世銀上調經濟展望；印度央行近四年來首度升息 
(1)世界銀行 2026 年 10 月《東亞與太平洋地區經濟展望報告》指出，受人工智慧
（AI） 相關出口與投資強勁帶動，將2026 年東亞與太平洋地區 （EAP） 經濟成長
率預測上修 0.3 個百分點至 4.5%，2027 年與 2028 年將分別放緩至 4.4%與 4.3%，
其中，越南、馬來西亞、泰國 GDP 大幅上調至 7.4%、5.1%及 2.0%。世銀指出，
AI 相關商品占多數經濟體出口增長的一半以上，其中，馬來西亞、菲律賓、泰國
和越南的 AI 出口貢獻率更超過七成，但扣除 AI 相關產品後，區域貿易成長呈現
疲弱或負增長，警告該地區的經濟韌性高度依賴 AI 相關製造業和出口，使其容
易受到全球科技支出逆轉的影響，建議應將 AI 產品生產優勢轉化為廣泛應用，
以提升生產力，並創造更多優質就業機會。此外，美伊戰爭推升能源價格，各國
政府比其他地區更積極因應 ， 也更仰賴補貼 ， 此將增加財政成本並減少外匯存底 ，
世銀預估中東石油出口要到 2027 年中才會恢復至戰前水準，能源成本上升已對
製造業造成壓力 ， 運輸價格大漲也已打擊消費需求 ， 持續性的補貼可能難以為繼 。

(2)印度:印度央行 10/7 貨幣政策會議宣布升息 1 碼至 5.5%，為近四年來首度升
息，貨幣政策立場從「中性」調整為「有節制的緊縮」。央行總裁指出，目前已
有跡象顯示，通膨預期升高，且價格壓力正逐漸擴散至更廣泛的領域。經濟展望
方面，印度內需、投資及基礎建設支出持續支撐景氣，央行將 2026/27 財年經濟
成長率預測由 6.7%上調至 7.1%，另受中東局勢緊張、國際油價上漲、食品價格
攀升及氣候因素影響，將 2026/27 財年 CPI 通膨率預測上調至 5.2%，高於 4%的
中期目標。另外，世界銀行 10 月最新《南亞經濟展望》報告，上修印度 2026/27
財年經濟成長率 0.4 個百分點至 7.1%，反映內需消費、製造業、服務業及出口表
現優於預期，然而，中東衝突導致能源價格上漲、聖嬰現象衝擊農業生產、全球
金融市場波動及資本流動風險，仍可能影響經濟前景。整體而言，印度經濟成長
動能仍穩健，但外部風險及輸入性通膨壓力增加，未來貨幣政策將以維持物價穩
定為優先，並有進一步升息的可能。 
 (3)越南: 2026 年第三季經濟成長年增率達 9.95%，高於上季修正後的 8.81%，並
創 4 年新高，累計前三季經濟成長率達 9.01%。成長動能主要來自製造業與營建
業擴張、外人直接投資持續流入、以及外貿大幅成長。各產業均穩健成長，工業
及建築業成長 12.50%(占 GDP 總值 51.57%)；服務業成長 9.54%(占 43.29%)；農
業成長 4.21%(占 5.14%)。以支出面觀之，民間消費成長 8.96%，固定投資成長
21.39%，貨物和服務出、進口分別成長23.27%、28.75%。展望未來，越南受惠於
全球企業分散供應鏈、AI 產業需求及政府推動基礎建設與數位轉型 ， 經濟前景仍
偏正向。

六、台灣—出口動能強勁，物價壓力與貿易風險並存 
9 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率升至 2.73%，創近兩年半新高，已連續 5 個
月高於 2%警戒線，主要係受豪雨影響，蔬果供應減少帶動價格上漲，加上汽機
車減稅效應淡化、中秋節落點不同及國際油價維持高檔，共同推升物價。除蔬果
外，外食費、房租、電費及交通相關費用皆持續上漲，顯示物價壓力仍在，但主
計總處認為整體漲勢仍屬溫和，預估 10 月 CPI 將略為回落至約 2.5%。 
9 月出口額達 872.2 億美元，年增 60.9%，其中資通產品出口倍增、電子零組
件年增 45.3%。財政部指出，在AI 相關投資持續、部分電子產品供應偏緊、新品
上市與旺季拉貨等因素共同挹注下，出口表現明顯升溫；隨著 AI 應用擴大及年
底採購需求增加，全年出口額有望突破 9,000 億美元。 
美國國際貿易委員會 （USITC）於10 月 9 日依據 《1930 年關稅法》 第337 條
款，對特定垂直供電系統及相關產品啟動專利侵權調查，其中台達電、廣達及鴻
海旗下相關企業等遭列名。若 337 條款調查最終認定侵權，相關產品可能遭禁止
輸入美國，後續應關注調查結果及對台灣電源供應與伺服器供應鏈的影響。

Potential FCC Rules on Optical
Transceivers More Likely to
Come in at 3.2T
Recent DC meetings highlight 3.2T as the most likely generation
to see restrictions on China-based optical transceivers. This
echoes conversations with LITE during the Q noting that any
restrictions would need to be phased in. See potential rules
keeping laser market tight, a pos for LITE/COHR.
Morgan Stanley & Co. LLC

Morgan Stanley MUFG Securities Co., Ltd.+
Yu Shirakawa
Equity Analyst
Yu.Shirakawa@morganstanleymufg.com                                                +81 3 6836-5432
Telecom & Networking Equipment
North America
Industry View In-Line
What Happened: Morgan Stanley strategists recently  met with a senior Washington
telecom, FCC and export-control legal team with direct engagement across the
White House and FCC. Discussion focused on the Administration’s efforts to reduce

reliance on Chinese technology, with the FCC Covered List emerging as the more
likely near-term mechanism for optical transceivers (a note we made in our AI Safety
DC trip wrap-up last week). Policy discussions suggest 3.2T transceivers would likely
be where restrictions would be phased in, potentially as soon as October, with
industry recently asked to demonstrate credible U.S. or friend-shoring plans before
3.2T becomes broadly deployed. Our conversations noted that discussions are also

indicating Chinese-built transceivers could still be importable, even at 3.2T, as long
as 65% of the BOM comes from US-based companies (DSP / laser would get you
pretty close). Initial 3.2T volumes are not expected to ramp in volume until 2029,
limiting the near-term revenue impact of a restriction even if action comes sooner.
We would see these rules as having the potential to avoid creating a constraint
on hyperscalers' ability to build today, but keeping the  laser market tighter given

laser would largely need to be from a US-based vendor to meet a 65% value
threshold, a positive for LITE/COHR.
How this echoes conversation with LITE earlier in the quarter: We hosted LITE at
Nasdaq Asia in late August, where we asked the company about potential FCC rules.
At the time they noted that the FCC had been in contact with them about potential
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Recent DC trip by our strategist   team gave a greater idea of what potential FCC
rules on optical transceivers could look like.
Potential restrictions, likely to come in at 3.2T, limit Chinese-based transceivers,
with a potential workaround if US-based content above 65% of the BOM.
Solution seemingly a win-win for LITE/COHR, with laser market likely to stay

tight, but not having to take on lower module assembly business.
Also, does not create bottlenecks for hyperscalers.
Assuring InP substrate delivery will be key to look at going forward.

Update
2
rules, with the company noting their feedback was that the rules would need to be
phased in over time to prevent material supply/demand dislocation in the market
with 60-65% of transceivers coming from China-based transceiver vendors. They
also noted that pure transceiver volumes alone, at current pricing structures, would
not be the most attractive business for them to bring on. These rules, which are
both phased in and don't limit Chinese assemblers, as long as enough content is

US-based, would seemingly address both of these concerns.
Our Thoughts:
1. 3.2T looks like the likely policy pressure point, but the near-term impact
should be limited - Administration efforts appear focused on reshaping the
supply chain before 3.2T reaches volume rather than disrupting the existing
800G and 1.6T base. With the 3.2T ramp not expected to begin until 2028
and broader adoption closer to 2029, an October Covered List action would
provide roughly two years before meaningful volumes enter the market.

Hyperscalers are already working on plans intended to demonstrate greater
U.S. or friend-shored manufacturing, suggesting the policy objective is
increasingly about determining where the next generation is built rather
than immediately displacing current Chinese supply.
2. Laser pricing looks like the stronger read-through versus transceiver
share - A Covered List framework does not necessarily require Chinese
module vendors to lose significant share if they can increase the U.S.

content of the underlying BOM. Combining a U.S. DSP from Marvell with
laser content from LITE/COHR could potentially move a Chinese-built
transceiver to the 65% U.S.-value threshold discussed in our strategists
team policy meeting. Such an outcome would leave Innolight and Eoptolink
(both covered by Andy Meng) with less flexibility to substitute toward
China-based lasers while allowing them to remain relevant at the module
level, reducing the risk from China-based laser makers. We therefore see a

stronger read-through for high-speed laser pricing and LITE/COHR
component share than for a wholesale redistribution of transceiver share.
3. Replacement capacity not ready; nor is it the most attractive opportunity
- As noted above, LITE noted that assembly of transceivers (mid-30's to low-
40s gross margin product), is not the most attractive opportunity for US-
based optical companies (vs. EMLs and lasers well north of 50% steady
state and approaching 75% today). While 3.2T is not yet in production, we

use AAOI's current 800G and 1.6T capacity ramp as an illustration of the
scale of US supply available to absorb a potential future demand shift. AAOI
(not covered), the only material US-based production of transceivers, is
currently ramping capacity. AAOI exited Q2 with manufacturing capacity
approaching 200,000 800G and 1.6T units per month and expects to reach
~650,000 units per month by year-end 2026, while management continues
to expect demand to outpace production through mid-2027. AAOI’s YE26

capacity annualizes to ~12M 800G/1.6T units, versus ~101M units
combined for Innolight and Eoptolink based on annualized 1H capacity
(44.3M and 56.7M, respectively), or >8x AAOI. Based on AAOI’s year-end
capacity plans and our Internet team’s hyperscaler capex work, we estimate
AAOI could support less than 4 GW of hyperscaler capacity even before

### Page 3

M
Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
factoring in manufacturing utilization. Replacing a meaningful portion of
Innolight and Eoptolink’s supply would therefore require a substantial multi-
year capacity build, with limited ability for existing U.S. suppliers to absorb
those volumes in the near term.
4. InP supply and sourcing outside of China will still be areas to watch -
Potential FCC rules could create a win-win for LITE/COHR by reducing the
risk of China-based laser makers flooding the market, without forcing them

to build significant transceiver module capacity to absorb lower-margin
assembly business. Were China to view the rules as too one-sided, it could
respond with tighter restrictions on InP substrate exports, a key bottleneck
to laser capacity ramping. At the same time, sourcing outside of China could
broaden the beneficiary set beyond U.S. suppliers, supporting InP substrate
demand at JX Advanced Metals (5016 JP), while providing a tailwind for
Sumitomo Electric’s (5802 JP) CWLD products and Furukawa Electric’s

(5801 JP) DFB lasers.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.

指数  51.7（+1.3）升至年内最高，新订单  50.5（-0.1），显示产出走强，但需求韧性仍
待强化。新出口订单小幅回落至  50.0  荣枯线，外需未继续上冲但对总体景气形成托底，
弱于8月的继续回升势头。购进价格  60.8（+4.2）  显著高于出厂价格  54.0（+3.6），“剪
刀差”扩大至约  +6.8pct  ，成本推动主导下，中下游利润修复的可持续性仍受约束。原材

料库存  48.2（+0.1）和产成品库存  47.6（-0.8）  均低于荣枯线，去库延续。消费品制造
与新动能行业需求更强，高耗能行业PMI仍低于  50  。从业人员  48.4（-0.3）  ，制造业企业
用工景气回落，侧面验证需求端修复仍偏弱。生产经营活动预期  53.8（持平）  ，供应商
配送时间  50.1（持平）  ，显示企业对四季度持谨慎乐观态度，交付效率稳定。非制造业
商务活动指数  50.2（+1.2）  ，同步返回扩张区间。新订单指数升至  46.5（+2.4）  ，环比
回升结束下行，显示需求修复迹象。总体来看，9月PMI显示整体经济景气水平有所改
善，但分项信号表明这轮回升的内生基础尚不牢固，内需修复仍需持续观察。

Document 133: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가현저한 저평가 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가현저한 저평가 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-06

Status: ok

### Page 1

00

삼성전자 (005930/KS) 
현저한 저평가 
3Q26 영업이익 106 조원 예상 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익을 106조원 (+18% QoQ)으로 예상한다. 이는 기존 당사 
전망치 111 조원 대비 4.5% 하향된 것이다. 원/달러 환율 가정을 기존 1,450원에서 
1,400원으로 3.4% 하향, MX/NW 사업부의 적자 폭을 확대 반영 (-0.5조원 -> -
1.4조원)했기 때문이다. 주가의 핵심인 메모리 판가는 DRAM +17%, NAND +15%
로 오히려 예상을 소폭 상회할 것으로 예상된다. 출하는 수요 강세에도 불구하고 재고 
부족, HBM 확대에 따른 제한적 공급 여력으로 DRAM +4%, NAND +7%의 기존 예
상을 유지한다. HBM은 분기 최대 출하를 경신하기 시작할 것이다. 또한 AI 강세로 파
운드리 수주 및 가동률의 동반 상승이 지속 중이다. 성과 충당금 반영 등에 따라 단기 
흑자전환 가시성은 제한적이나, DS 사업부 전체적으로 강화되고 있는 국면이다.

2027 년 568 조원 (+55% YoY) 전망 유지. 환율 하락 반영 
SK증권은 삼성전자의 2027년 영업이익 전망치를 568조원으로 유지한다. 원/달러 환
율 가정을 기존 1,440원에서 1,340원으로 7% 하향했음에도, 실적 전망을 유지하는 
이유는 AI 메모리 수요 강세 속 공급 부족 심화, LTA에 따른 Dual market 효과에 의
한 Commodity 메모리 가격 강세, 2027년 HBM 판가 강세 (+103% YoY) 와 출하 
강세, 수익성 회복의 동반, 파운드리 적자 폭 축소의 본격화 전망 때문이다. 2027년 메
모리 출하는 DRAM +22%, NAND +16%, 판가는 DRAM +38%, NAND +28%
를 예상한다. 시장에서 우려하는 메모리 가격 하락, 감익 시나리오는 없을 것이다.

차별적 실적과 주주환원 본격화. 현저한 저평가 
SK증권은 메모리 업황과 삼성전자의 주가가 현저히 저평가 되어있다고 판단한다. 삼성
전자의 2027E P/E는 4.2X에 불과하며, 이익 창출력은 글로벌 최상위권이다. 주주환
원 정책 또한 기존의 FCF 50% 환원이 향후에도 유지될 것으로 가정한다면, 2027년 
193조원, 2028년 222조원으로 현재 시가총액 (우선주 포함) 대비 각각 11%, 13% 
수준에 육박한다. 성장을 위한 투자를 확대하고 있음에도, 대규모의 환원이 가능하다. 
LTA는 AI 시대 메모리 수요의 질과 실적 가시성을 제고시키며, 이는 주주환원의 지속
가능성을 의미한다. 메모리 가격의 높은 기저, LTA 물량의 계약 가격 적용을 감안하면, 
가격 상승률 자체는 안정화될 것이다. 다만 실적 안정성, 가시성, Duration을 확보하기 
시작했다는 점이 훨씬 중요한 가치이며, 아직 시장의 Playbook에 없을 뿐이다. 시대는 
변하고 있고, 시장도 변해야 한다. 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 610,000원을 유지한다. 
2026-10-06

반도체. 한동희 / donghee.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-8826

Document 141: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

00

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 
기다렸던 4 행정 중속 엔진 캐파 증설 단행 
HD현대중공업의 4행정 엔진 캐파는 기존 3.0GW에서 증설이 완료되는 '28년부터 
램프업을 거쳐 '30년 7.2GW까지 확대될 계획이다. ①[육상 발전용]은 신공장(울산 
온산) 3GW 와 HD 현대엔진(전남 영암) 1GW 를 합쳐 4.0GW, ②[선박용]은 
2.3GW에서 3.2GW로 확대된다. HD현대엔진 캐파는 기존 0.7GW에서 1GW까
지 확대되며, 해당 라인은 신공장이 가동되기 전 ‘27~’28 년 구간에서의 선박&육상 엔
진 모두 대응 가능하다. 신공장 캐파는 총 3.0GW로, ‘28년 약 30%의 가동률로 시
작해 램프업을 거쳐 ‘30 년부터 풀캐파로 20MW급 모델을 생산할 예정이다. 4 행정 
중속엔진에 대한 수요 가시성은 높다. 동사가 공유했던 육상 발전 엔진 인콰이어리 규
모는 기존 약 3~9GW였으나, 현재는 이를 크게 상회하는 수준으로 소통하고 있다.

최근 부유식 데이터센터(FDC)에 대한 하이퍼스케일러들의 인콰이어리 또한 확대되고 
있다. HD 한국조선해양은 최근 미국 선급(ABS)로부터 100MW급 규모의 부유식 발
전 설비(BMPP)에 대한 기본 인증(AiP)를 획득했으며, 동사는 FDC 신조 사업과 더불
어 발전원 4 행정 엔진을 파워십 및 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)에 납품하며 그룹사들과 
패키지 사업으로 진행할 예정이다. 가시화된 수요를 기반으로 엔진기계사업부의 구조적 
성장 보여줄 것으로 전망하며, 이에 따른 리레이팅을 예상한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원 유지 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원을 유지한다. 동사는 
올해 상선수주 목표 이미 초과달성하며 안정적인 펀더멘털 유지 중이다. 하반기 남은 
기간은 LNGC, VLGC/VLAC 중심의 선별 수주 전략을 통해 ‘28 년 이후의 상선 부
문 수익성 유지 충분히 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 글로벌 지정학적 리스크 확대에 따른 
해군 현대화 사이클 기조에 맞추어 미 해군은 동맹국의 호위함 구매를 고려하고 있으
며, MASGA를 기반으로 중장기적으로 미국 전투함을 건조하기 위해 HD현대의 미국 
진출 가능성 높다는 판단이다. 또한 미국을 제외한 글로벌 함정(페루, 필리핀, 말레이시
아, 칠레, 사우디, 이집트 등) 수주 파이프라인도 다수 보유하고 있어 함정 모멘텀에 따
른 리레이팅 충분히 가능할 전망이다. 특수선+4 행정 엔진+FDC+SMR 등 조선소에 
붙일 수 있는 모든 추가 성장동력을 보유한 유일한 종목이기에 조선업 최선호주(Top-
Pick)로 제시한다. 최근 불거지고 있는 노조 파업 문제 해소될 시 저점 매수 기회라는 
판단이다.

2026-09-17

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

HD현대중공업 수정TP(원)

Document 157: AI模型遭遇消费降级：顶级模型使用占比从55%降至14%，用户转向性价比

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI模型遭遇消费降级：顶级模型使用占比从55%降至14%，用户转向性价比.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

AI ⼤模型竞争正从追求极致性能转向注重性价⽐。 OpenRouter 数据显⽰，前⼗款⾼性能模
型的使⽤占⽐从 2025 年⼀季度的 55% 降⾄ 2026 年三季度的 14% 。 Meta 此时推出 Muse
Wonder ，试图证明⾃⾝的前沿模型实⼒，但性能领先能否转化为实际⽤⼾需求，仍有待市
场检验。
摘要：
AI ⼤模型⾏业正⾯临⼀个反差：科技巨头仍在竞相推出性能更强的前沿模型，⽤⼾却开始转向更
便宜、⾜以满⾜实际需求的产品。 Meta 此时推出 Muse Wonder ，试图证明⾃⾝的前沿模型实
⼒，但市场需求正从追求极致性能转向注重性价⽐。
据 The Information 近⽇报道，通过 OpenRouter 调⽤的 AI 模型中，使⽤量排名前⼗的⾼性能模型
占⽐已从 2025 年第⼀季度的 55% 降⾄ 2026 年第三季度的 14% 。随着低成本模型的能⼒不断提
升，越来越多⽤⼾发现，完成⽇常任务并不需要调⽤最昂贵的模型。
这意味着， AI ⾏业的竞争正在从单纯⽐拼模型性能，转向⽐拼性能、成本与实际应⽤价值。对于

Meta ⽽⾔，即使 Muse Wonder 能够在特定任务上达到领先⽔平，也仍需证明这种性能优势能够
转化为⽤⼾需求。
“ 前沿 ” 标签泛滥，模型性能竞赛仍在升温
近期，主要 AI 公司密集推出旗舰模型，并强调其技术领先性。⾕歌推出 Gemini 4 Argon ，称其在
多个领域的复杂⼯作流中达到前沿⽔平； OpenAI 发布 GPT-6 Astra ，宣称其在计算机操作、软件
⼯程、⽹络安全及专业⼯作等领域达到最先进⽔平； Anthropic 则推出 Claude Fable 5.1 和
Mythos 5.1 ，将其定位为全球领先的编程与知识⼯作模型。
Meta 推出 Muse Wonder ，也是在这⼀背景下重新强调⾃⾝的技术竞争⼒。经历约 14 个⽉的⼤规
模招聘扩张后，公司希望借助新模型证明⾃⼰仍处于 AI 前沿。不过，当各家⼚商都在强调性能
领先时， “ 前沿 ” 本⾝越来越难以成为差异化优势。
据报道， Meta 计划将 Muse Wonder ⽤于⽀持其个⼈智能体产品 Muse 。但新模型的技术表现能否

转化为⽤⼾可感知的体验提升，以及这种提升是否⾜以⽀撑更⾼的使⽤成本，仍有待验证。
低成本模型崛起，⽤⼾更看重性价⽐
OpenRouter 的数据揭⽰了需求端的变化：⾼性能模型的使⽤占⽐⼤幅下降，反映出⽤⼾正在重
新权衡模型能⼒与调⽤成本。随着低成本模型不断进步，许多⽇常任务已不再需要最昂贵的模
型。
企业尤其倾向于根据任务难度分配模型：常规⼯作交给价格更低的模型，只有复杂推理、专业
分析等⾼要求任务才调⽤顶级模型。这种做法既能满⾜实际需求，也能控制 AI ⽀出。
消费者市场也⾯临类似变化。对普通⽤⼾⽽⾔，模型能否准确回答问题、顺利完成任务，往往
⽐其是否达到⾏业性能榜单的顶端更重要。当 “ ⾜够好⽤ ” 的模型能够以更低价格满⾜⼤部分需求
时，顶级模型即使持续刷新性能纪录，也未必能获得相应的使⽤增⻓。
对 Meta 等科技巨头⽽⾔，挑战已不只是推出更强的模型，还在于证明性能提升能够带来真实的
⽤⼾需求。在 AI 竞争从追逐性能转向衡量性价⽐的过程中，技术领先与商业成功之间的关系正在
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

⻛险提⽰及免责条款

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
• Any forecast rests on assumptions. Each might be
individually reasonable, but if they all tilt in the same
direction, they look less plausible in combination.
• Different assumptions can also drive different conclusions,
as can be seen in Treasury and RBA views on trend growth

in supply capacity. Treasury’s long-term view on both
productivity growth and labour force participation trends is
systematically less downbeat than the RBA’s (shorter-term)
view. Treasury’s estimate of the stable-inflation rate of
unemployment is also lower than the RBA’s estimate.
• We consider the risks around the RBA’s view to be
asymmetric. The RBA’s assumptions implicitly require a
repeat of the factors that dragged recent productivity
outcomes down, despite strong global investment, policy

changes domestically and the possible benefits of AI. Some
observers appear to extrapolate this weakness beyond the
RBA’s forecast horizon. But this would be hard to square
with the reasonable belief that the global interest rate
structure will be higher in future than it was pre-pandemic.
A systematic tilt, even if unintentional and understandable,
may point to the likely direction of future surprises.
Any forecast rests on assumptions, and the further out

you forecast, the harder it is to ground those assumptions
in something real. Add the uncertainty about AI, where
predictions range from “unbounded prosperity without
needing to work” to “we will all be dead in a couple of years”,
and you can see why assuming the future looks like the past is
an attractive fall-back position.
How much of the past, though? This is the context for the
debate about the Intergenerational Review (IGR)  released this
week. In one corner, Treasury assumes that future productivity

growth will average 1.2%yr over the next 40 years, in line with
the 30-year average, and that labour force participation will
continue to trend up, as it has for the past five decades, until
2040. In the other corner, the RBA assumes that productivity
growth will average 0.7%yr, in line with the past 20 years
– essentially the post-GFC and pandemic period – and that
participation rates will be flat to down in the near term.
Both productivity assumptions have limitations: round-number-

of-year averages might be distorted by capturing a downturn
without the matching upturn, or vice versa. Peak-to-peak or
trough-to-trough is always preferable. Even that calculation
cannot escape the weakness of recent years, though.
When your assumptions have only upside risk
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
To be fair, the RBA’s productivity assumptions relate only to
the short term and are not strictly comparable to the IGR,
as the Governor has emphasised this week.  By contrast, the

near-term divergence in forecasts for the participation rate,
which my colleague Ryan Wells  first highlighted in May , is a
genuine divergence.
The RBA’s more downbeat set of assumptions could be
unwittingly contributing to a self-fulfilling talking-down
of Australia’s prospects that clearly appeals to some
commentators outside the public sector. Many in this group
view productivity growth as stemming wholly or mostly from
policy “reforms”, harking back to the halcyon Hawke–Keating

era of deregulation. Proponents point to the lack of reform
recently to justify this downbeat view of growth capacity. We
think this misses the global nature of that slowdown.
Part of the trouble with the downbeat view of future
productivity is that it implicitly assumes that the factors
weighing on productivity growth in the past will continue.
But there are good reasons not to expect the same post-GFC
weakness in risk appetite, and so investment and capital

accumulation, decades later and absent another crisis. The AI
boom and associated resurgence of risk appetite make it hard
to argue that we will see the same lack of new productivity-
enhancing technologies and general malaise as that period.
For Australia specifically, assuming the same low average
productivity growth as the past 20 years would require
assuming a compositional drag s imilar to a repeat of the
run-up of the care economy and decline in measured mining

production over the past 15 years. To be fair, decarbonisation
does imply further declines in (capital-intensive, high-
productivity) coal production, dragging mining productivity
down further. It would be a stretch, though, to assume
expansion in critical and transition minerals provides no offset.
We can grant that AI’s impact on productivity will take a while
to come through and still expect some boost in coming years
– at least by the end of the RBA’s forecast horizon in 2028.

Indeed, some observers already see an AI-induced boost to
service-sector activity in the recent upside surprise in UK GDP.
Some productivity pessimists might want to argue that the
“RBA assumptions collectively imply a
downbeat view of supply growth."

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
rest of the world will benefit from the AI boom, but somehow
not Australia, or not until later. Such an extraordinary claim
requires a mechanism explaining that divergence, though.
The Productivity Commission estimated back in December
that AI could boost multifactor productivity by 2.3%pt over

the course of the next decade, or roughly a quarter-point per
year extra growth on average – a big difference in the scheme
of usual growth rates in output and productivity. Labour
productivity growth would be boosted a little more; the data
centre boom is expanding the capital stock. But an estimate
published in December is unlikely to adequately capture the
step change in capability of the main models, especially the
coding and agentic harnesses, that emerged over the summer

of 2025/26. Given what we understand to be limited adoption
of AI in the Australian public sector, policymakers might also
be systematically underestimating both the technology’s
capabilities and the breadth of uptake in the private sector.
As an illustration of how downbeat you need to be to
extrapolate the RBA’s near-term assumption further out,
suppose that all AI does is make about 10% of the economy
10% more productive over four years. This extra 1% of

economy-wide productivity is well below most estimates
of the impact. It would nonetheless add a quarter-point on
annual productivity growth for four years, bringing a downbeat
0.7% assumption up to almost 1%.
A deeper challenge is that, beyond the RBA’s near-term
horizon, low productivity growth is hard to square with a
higher future average for interest rates globally.
We note, for example, recent comments by RBA Deputy
Governor Hauser, pointing to 700 years of bond yield data

to suggest that the post-GFC period of low interest rates
was an anomaly, something we noted two years ago. The
global structure of interest rates emerges to balance global
saving and investment, and stronger investment for AI and
other purposes is clearly in evidence. We find it implausible
to assume that all that investment happens, pushing up
global yields, and none of it delivers higher productivity. Even
increased defence spending has historically had productivity

payoffs, as Ukraine’s drone manufacturing ecosystem has
exemplified recently.
It is understandable that when inflation is above target, a
central bank would rather err on the side of caution than
techno-optimism. They know that being downbeat, and
running policy a little too tight, means a faster return of
inflation to target, which is no bad thing for an inflation-
targeting central bank.
It matters, though, when those understandable assumptions

are systematically one-sided. If there are arguments that
productivity growth over the next few years will be even lower
than the 0.7%yr 20-year average, we are yet to see them. In
contrast, there are reasonable arguments for it to be a bit higher.
Similarly, the RBA’s participation rate assumptions are low
given near-term demographic trends , and out of step with
Treasury’s revised projections. Today’s labour market data
for August shows the participation rate rising again, to just

12½ basis points below the January 2025 all-time high. This
supports the idea that RBA’s view on participation trends
is too downbeat. The IGR’s NAIRU (stable-inflation rate of
unemployment) assumption is a quarter-point or so below
RBA estimates revealed in various FOI requests and the
Governor’s comments this week.
Together, the three RBA assumptions imply weaker growth
in supply capacity and thus more inflationary pressure for any
given level of demand. If every one of your assumptions seems

individually plausible, but all skew in the one direction, it
starts to look like a pattern. The thumb on the scale is unlikely
to be intentional, but it does suggest the likely direction of
future surprises in the medium term.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: the importance of supply
This week, market participants have focused on discussions
between the US and China on trade, security and technology
as President Xi visits Washington.
Preliminary discussions between US Treasury Secretary

Bessent and Chinese Vice Premier He Lifeng ahead of
President Xi’s arrival in Washington were characterised by
both sides as productive, and Treasury Secretary Bessent
subsequently announced that discussions in Washington
would leave trade negotiations for upcoming meetings
between the two leaders in Shenzhen and Miami. To allow
room for this deferral, the current trade truce will be extended
until January 2027. President Trump subsequently outlined

that discussions during President Xi’s Washington visit would
instead focus on security, technology and AI.
A more constructive tone was also seen between the US and
Iran overnight, if press reports are accurate. According to
Bloomberg and Reuters, sources suggest the US and Iran are
again exploring a staged deal which would see Iran re-open
the Strait of Hormuz and the US lift its blockade of Iranian
ports. Negotiations are occurring on the sidelines of the UN

General Assembly, with Qatari officials mediating.
President Trump continues to show little interest in a deal
before November’s mid-term elections, but the global
economy is under increasing pressure as the price of Brent oil
hovers between USD100 and USD110 while refining spreads
add additional impetus to refined product prices. The mooted
consideration of a potential US diesel export ban this week
makes clear that the US is not immune from this pressure

despite having significant domestic energy supply and refining
capacity, relative to their own demand.
The consequences of the current energy supply shock and other
disruptions the US have experienced over the past year remain
front of mind for FOMC members. Those speaking this week such
as NY and Cleveland Fed Presidents Williams and Hammack
highlighted the risk to inflation expectations from a series of
supply shocks when the economy is fully employed, and the

likelihood of an additional hike(s) being required to rein in current
inflation momentum and risks. Williams noted that expectations
of another hike by year end are "reasonable" and that the FOMC
have "a lot of work to do". Debt market’s concern remains acute,
having now almost priced in four hikes over the coming year and
taken the US 10-year above 5.00%, currently 5.20%.
Australia’s only data release this week was the August
Labour Force Survey . Employment surprised to the upside,

rising 39.5k. But, once again, new labour demand was more
than offset by supply as the participation rate bounced
0.2ppts higher to 67.1%, just shy of January 2025’s 115-year
high of 67.2%. As a result, the unemployment rate increased
from 4.5% to 4.6% and was close to rounding up to 4.7%
(4.65% to two decimal places).
There may be some residual seasonality in August’s print
related to the new survey collection model. However, the
underlying trend is still consistent with a gradual easing in the

labour market, seeing the unemployment rate drift higher. We
expect this to continue through to the first half of 2027.
The strength in labour force participation also speaks to the
broader issue of Australia’s supply capacity. As discussed
by Chief Economist Luci Ellis , the RBA’s assumptions around
participation, productivity and the NAIRU collectively
skew towards a weak assessment of supply capacity. For
participation in particular, the RBA’s near-term assumptions

are downbeat compared to recent demographic trends and
also sit below Treasury’s longer-term projections as set out in
the 2026 IGR. As above, the August LFS emphasises that slack
could emerge more quickly than the RBA’s forecasts imply
without an outright fall in employment.
Ahead of next week’s RBA meeting and inflation data,
our latest state-level analysis  provides perspective on the
persistence of underlying price pressures, particularly the

importance of housing-related components. For a broader
assessment of the fiscal backdrop and the implications for the
states, our latest state fiscal update  explores how the housing
market correction and changes to NDIS funding arrangements
are pressuring state finances.
Before turning to this week’s events offshore, it is important
to highlight that last Friday the Bank of Japan raised its
policy rate by 25bp to 1.25%. Policymakers maintained a

constructive assessment of the economy, pointing to resilient
consumption, ongoing labour market tightness, and solid
investment spending. More importantly, the Bank expressed
growing confidence that inflation is increasingly being
sustained by domestic wage and demand dynamics rather
than imported cost shocks alone.
In our view, the emphasis placed on improving underlying
inflation dynamics suggests policymakers are becoming
increasingly comfortable that inflation is able to be sustained by

domestic demand and wage growth. Normalisation is likely to
remain gradual, but the Bank appears firmly on a path towards
further tightening, with rates likely to reach 1.5% by mid-2027.
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
Along for the ride
There was plenty of action in the markets this week, as global
interest rates continued to move higher, and New Zealand
was taken along for the ride. In the US, strong economic data
and resurgent oil prices have led the market to believe that

the central bank will need to get on with the job and hike
its policy rate further in the months ahead. Longer-term US
interest rates have continued to lift sharply, driving both a rise
in domestic interest rates and a fall in the New Zealand dollar,
with the trade-weighted index already 4% below what the
RBNZ assumed in its September Monetary Policy Statement.
While the September MPS worked to dampen market
expectations about the pace of future rate hikes, offshore

developments have seen market pricing for the OCR bounce back
and exceed previous levels. Interest rate markets are currently
pricing around a 75% chance of a hike at the 28 October policy
review, with a high chance of another hike on top of this at the 9
December review. We’d question whether the RBNZ’s thinking
has really shifted to that degree in the last few weeks though.
Notably, the RBNZ provided some unscheduled comments on
the monetary policy outlook this week, in what was otherwise

a very light local news week. In connection with a visit to
Dunedin by Governor Breman and other RBNZ leaders, the
RBNZ put out a media release that included the following:
“If higher oil prices persist, they are expected to result in
somewhat higher near-term inflation than we assumed in the
September Statement… As always, we will assess incoming
data and global developments ahead of our next decision in
October and remain focused on the outlook for inflation over

the medium term.”
Both of these statements are fairly benign and factual in
themselves. What’s more notable is that the RBNZ felt the
need to make a public comment at all; the occasion didn’t
require one. (The Governor’s presentation at a business lunch
was originally billed as being based on material from the
September MPS, with no new insights.)
If the intention was to guide market expectations for the
October meeting lower, it wasn’t entirely successful. Indeed,

the market initially fixated on the first part of the statement –
higher near-term inflation – and pushed pricing for an October
hike even higher on the day. While this was unwound the
following day, market pricing for OCR hikes remains higher
than it was at the start of the week.
As it stands, that leaves the RBNZ with the prospect of
rattling the market if it follows through with the October
pause that it signalled in September MPS. However, it’s

possible that the local and offshore economic data, together
with geopolitical developments, could shift market pricing in
the meantime. Domestically, the NZIER’s quarterly survey of
business opinion (6 Oct) and the September quarter CPI (22
Oct) will be especially important in that regard.
Turning to the data, the only release this week was our
Employment Confidence Index, produced in conjunction with
McDermott Miller. Compared to our consumer confidence

survey, there was a relatively weak bounce in employment
confidence in the September quarter. The index rose by 3.2
points to 86.3, leaving it at the second-lowest level on record
for this survey going back to 2004.
Perceptions of current job opportunities were unchanged at a
net -60%. This is the component of the survey that seems to
have the most predictive power for near-term labour market
conditions and is broadly consistent with the unemployment

rate holding at its current 11-year high of 5.6%. Hiring has
remained soft to an unusual degree for this stage of the
economic cycle, and those who are out of the workforce have
been finding it increasingly difficult to get in.
It’s clear that there is still substantial slack in the labour
market and little upward pressure on wages, which would
push back somewhat against the market’s view that the RBNZ
will need to pick up the pace and deliver two more OCR hikes

before Christmas. The RBNZ doesn’t need to have employment
formally in its mandate for it to be attentive to the signals
coming from the labour market.
The data flow picks up again next week, with monthly releases
for employment, building activity, and business and consumer
confidence – see our previews on the following page. The
other release of note is the Pre-Election Fiscal and Economic
Update (PREFU) on Tuesday. This ‘book opening’ exercise
is designed to create a level playing field, allowing parties

outside the current Government and the public more generally
to judge to the state of the public finances in the leadup to the
General Election on 7 November. In the case of the opposition
parties, it will also allow them to calibrate the spending and
taxation policies on which they will campaign. See our preview
for the details on what we expect from this release.
Michael Gordon
Senior Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Aug Household Spending Indicator (%mth)
Sep 29, Last: 1.1%, WBC f/c: –0.5%
Mkt f/c: 0.3%, Range: –0.5% to 0.7%
The household spending indicator rose 1.1%mth in July,
comfortably exceeding expectations and extending the strong
momentum evident through the middle of the year. Annual

growth accelerated to 7.0%yr, the fastest pace since early
2023.
Our Westpac-DataX Card Tracker points to softer spending
conditions through August and early September, with higher
fuel prices increasingly driving spending growth and, at the
margin, squeezing spending elsewhere. The detail suggests
a modest rotation away from discretionary categories, with
hospitality spending easing and vehicle sales also likely
moderating after a strong June quarter. Together with a softer

read from our MHSI-aligned card measure, we anticipate the
household spending indicator will decline 0.5%mth in August.
AUS: RBA Policy Decision (%)
Sep 29, Last: 4.35, Westpac f/c: 4.60
Market f/c: 4.60, Range: 4.60
Westpac anticipates the RBA Monetary Policy Board will raise
the cash rate by 25bps to 4.60% at its September meeting.
As discussed by Chief Economist Luci Ellis recent rhetoric from
the RBA have escalated, particularly with Governor Bullock

flagging that upside inflation risks appear to be "materialising"
– the conditionality set in August for further rate hikes.
There is a risk of a follow-up hike, though it crucially depends
on the data flow after the meeting. For September, we
continue to expect a split vote at the meeting, with material
differences in opinion about trend growth in supply capacity,
as discussed on Page 2.
AUS: Aug Headline CPI (%mth)
Sep 30, Last: 1.0, Westpac f/c: 0.4

Market f/c: 0.5, Range: 0.4 to 0.9
The July CPI reversed May and June weakness with a larger
than expected 1.0% increase. This was stronger than
Westpac’s expectation of 0.8% and market’s expectation of
0.9%. The annual pace eased to 3.5%yr from 3.8%yr.
We are looking for a 0.4% increase in the August CPI. Food &
non-alcoholic beverages to rise 0.2%mth with solid gains for
bread & cereal products (0.4%), meat & seafood (0.5%) and
food products n.e.c. (0.4%) partially offset by falling non-

alcoholic beverages (–0.6%) and a soft print for meals out &
take away food (0.1%).
At two decimal points it is a 0.43% increase in August so the
risk this month is to the upside.
For more details please see “ August CPI Preview ”.

AUS: Aug Trimmed Mean CPI (%mth)
Sep 30, Last: 0.5, Westpac f/c: 0.2
Market f/c: 0.3, Range: 0.2 to 0.4
The Trimmed Mean (TM) lifted 0.5%mth/3.5%yr, on par with
last July which was the strongest monthly read in the series’
short history. The result was stronger than both our and the

market’s expectation of 0.4%mth.
August TM is expected to rise 0.2%mth lifting the annual pace
to 3.6%yr. For market services excluding volatiles and holiday
travel we have pencilled in a step down to 0.1%mth, this series
lifted 0.8% in July possibly due to the larger-than-expected
increase in minimum wages.
The distribution of the August TM is from a low of –0.32%mth
to a high of 0.53%mth. Remembering the TM is seasonally
adjusted, with rents (+0.17%mth) and dwellings (+0.20%mth)

mostly locking in the TM. See “ August CPI Preview ”.
AUS: Aug Dwelling Approvals (%mth)
Sep 30, Last: –3.6, Westpac f/c: –5.0
Market f/c: –1.0, Range: –6.0 to 1.0
Dwelling approvals fell 3.6%mth in July, with annual growth
easing to 9.0%yr. The decline was driven primarily by detached
dwellings, although unit approvals also edged lower. The
result suggests higher interest rates may be starting to weigh
on new housing demand.
We expect a similar story to play out in August, with

elevated interest rates continuing to weigh on detached
dwelling approvals, while the unit segment holds broadly
steady. Overall, dwelling approvals are expected to decline
5%mth. While risks remain tilted to the downside, residential
construction should remain relatively resilient through 2026,
supported by the strong lift in approvals through the first half
of the year and a sizeable pipeline of work yet to be done.
AUS: Aug Private Sector Credit (%mth)

Sep 30, Last: 0.6, Westpac f/c: 0.5
Market f/c: 0.5, Range: 0.3 to 0.6
Growth in private sector credit growth appeared unsustainable
over May-Jun, especially given the slowing in the housing
market. Come July, the pace now dropped from 0.8%mth to
0.6%mth. Housing credit the main source of weakness, with
investor credit growth beginning to adjust lower in response to
higher interest rates, lower house prices and, most importantly,
tax changes for investment properties. Meanwhile, business

borrowing continued to rise rapidly.
For August, we have pencilled in a 0.5%mth increase, and we
look for continued divergence between housing and business
segments.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Sep Cotality Home Value Index (%mth)
Oct 1, Last: –0.9, Westpac f/c: –0.9
The Cotality home value index – covering all of Australia – fell
0.9% in August following downwardly-revised 1.2% and 0.9%
declines in July and June. Annual growth slowed to 2.7%yr.

Prices nationally are now 3.6% below their peak in March.
The September update is set to see more of the same with
the daily measure (covering the five major capital cities rather
than all of Australia) tracking a 1.2% decline in line with
this measure’s August move. Annual growth looks like to
stall about flat. The detail suggests monthly declines have
accelerated in Brisbane, Perth and Adelaide but moderated a
touch in Melbourne, noting that there is a seasonal element

to price moves in Sep. Markets are still adjusting to higher
interest rates and the tax policy changes introduced in the May
Federal budget with more weakness likely into year-end.
AUS: Aug Goods Trade Balance ($bn)
Oct 1, Last: 1.9, Westpac f/c: 2.3
Market f/c: 2.2, Range: 1.5 to 2.5
The goods trade surplus narrowed slightly to $1.9bn in July
but remained above our and consensus expectations. This
was down from an upwardly revised surplus of $2.3bn in June.

Underneath the modest headline change was considerable
volatility among components, especially in flows of non-
monetary gold, remain the chief culprit.
For August, we have pencilled in a slight widening of the trade
surplus to $2.3bn. Port data points to a bounce-back in coal
and iron ore export volumes,  and prices looked to have edged
higher. However, volatility in non-monetary gold trade and
other hard-to-predict sub-categories (like ADP equipment)

remain a wildcard.
NZ: Aug Monthly Employment Indicator (%mth)
Sep 28, Last: 0.3, Westpac f/c: 0.3
The MEI has continued to tick higher in recent months,
showing that the jobs market has held its ground through
the Iran conflict and the consequent surge in fuel prices. The
weekly snapshots provided by Stats NZ have been running
ahead of year-ago levels and suggest another solid result
for August – we’ve pencilled in a 0.3% increase, following
a similar rise in July. However, the history of this indicator

suggests that both months are likely to be revised down a bit
from their initial release.

### Page 9

Nonfarm payrolls surprised to the upside in August, 
registering a gain of 162k with 55k in back revisions to June 
and July. The report left the 3-month average at 71k, near the 
top of the estimated range consitent with balance between 
labour demand and supply. Come September, the trend is 
expected to remain in place with a 80k gain forecast. With 
inflation risks paramount, upward revisions will have a greater 
impact on market variables than a negative revision.
The decline in the participation rate over the past year has 
capped the unemployment rate. Participation is likely to edge 
higher in September and take the unemployment rate up 
to 4.2% with it. There is little available slack in the labour 
market, but plenty just outside if demand lifts.

Marvell 營收翻⼗倍？ SpaceX 下場
做電信商？ Google 帶核電⾶？  - KP
思考筆記 ( 第 63 期 )
KP@FOMOSOC, BENITA HSU, AND ポニョ  オタク
OCT 10
READ IN APP
⼤家好，我是  KP ，歡迎來到第  63 期的《週末思考筆記》。
⼤概兩個⽉前，我開了⼀個新欄⽬，叫「財報解剖」。當時有跟⼤家講，這是
⼀個新嘗試，不保證更新頻率，先跑跑看。

兩個⽉下來，總共出了五篇，節奏慢慢穩了，我和團隊也越寫越順。所以今天
想正式跟⼤家宣佈：這個欄⽬會⼀直做下去。
也就是說， FOMO 研究院現在有三條線：
深⼊分析： ⼀個產業或公司主題，深挖到底，週中發出；

### Page 2

思考筆記： 相對短⼀點，⼀週的市場觀察與隨筆，週末發出；
財報解剖： ⽐較硬核，數字會多⼀點，在⼀家公司出財報之後發出。
講到這裡，我想多聊幾句我對這三條線的想法。
深⼊分析做了超過⼀年了。這⼀年多，我們⼀起⾛過了很多產業、很多公司，
有些可能你之前完全沒聽過，有些你可能聽過但沒認真看過。
我很喜歡這個過程，把⼀個陌⽣的東⻄拆開，搞懂它，再跟你講清楚。
但我最近開始有⼀個感覺：廣度鋪開了，是時候多⼀點往深度⾛。
財報解剖做的事情，其實跟深⼊分析不太⼀樣。
深⼊分析是帶你認識⼀個你可能不知道的公司或產業；財報解剖是挑⼀家⼤家
都聽過的公司，但我們不只看⼀次，我們持續看、反覆看、每個季度再看⼀
次。
我想做的事情是建⽴⼀個「追蹤名單」。你作為讀者，可以每個季度回來，看
看這家公司跟上⼀季⽐怎麼樣了、跟我們上次拆解的時候⽐怎麼樣了。
⽼實講，我⽬前還沒有把同⼀家公司寫過兩次財報解剖，所以這個「追蹤」的
感覺到底會變成什麼樣⼦，我⾃⼰也很期待，要跑起來才知道。
但⽅向是這樣的：⼀個覆蓋名單，慢慢成形，⼤家可以⼀起追蹤那些公司的進

展。
完整名單我還沒完全定下來，之後會慢慢跟⼤家揭曉。
我也不保證每⼀次財報我都會寫⼀篇完整的⻑⽂，有些季度可能真的沒什麼⼤
變化，但我希望⾄少會有⼀個短筆記，跟你講⼀聲：「嘿，我看了，⼤概是這
樣。」
這樣你不會斷線，我也能把脈搏持續摸著。
好，講完願景，講⼀下實際的節奏調整。
下週有兩家公司出財報（我不講名字，讓⼤家猜⼀下，但你查⼀下財報⽇曆⼤
概猜得到），我和團隊會集中⽕⼒準備，財報出來之後也會第⼀時間拆解。
所以下週，會有兩篇財報解剖，取代原本的深⼊分析。

### Page 3

往後也是這樣：如果某⼀周有⽐較多我們要覆蓋的財報，財報解剖可能會頂掉
那週的深⼊分析。每次有這個情況，我會提前跟⼤家講⼀聲。
我想強調的是，這不是說深⼊分析不重要，它依然是主菜。
只是跑了⼀年多，我們把地圖畫得夠⼤了，接下來我想把⼀些已經畫上地
圖的地⽅，再鑽深⼀點。
與其每週都在開新地圖，不如偶爾回到⼀個⽼朋友那裡，看看它這三個⽉
⻑成什麼樣⼦了。
做「深⼊分析」需要極⼤的腦⼒激盪，但我後來發現，其實⼤家的注意⼒也是
有限且寶貴的資源。
如果我們每週都在塞⼀個全新的、陌⽣的公司或產業，久了⼤家也會產⽣資訊
疲勞。「怎麼這週⼜是⼀個我沒聽過的名字？」。
所以，偶爾⽤⼤家熟悉的公司（財報解剖）來替換⼀下，不僅能讓我的研究團
隊有更充⾜的時間打磨品質，也能讓⼤家在閱讀時有更熟悉的錨點，讀起來更
輕鬆、更有共鳴，也有⼀種「喔，原來它變成這樣了」的樂趣。

這個星期我們有三個主題，但當中有兩個⽐較⻑：
主題⼀  | Marvell 說五年後營收會翻⼗倍？
主題⼆  | Google 簽約  Constellation Energy ，核電準備起⾶？
主題三  | SpaceX 準備下場做電信商？
不妨考慮先讚後看，我們開始 !

### Page 4

主題⼀  | Marvell 說五年後營收會翻
⼗倍？
Marvell 的投資⼈⽇上，管理層把  2031 年的營收⽬標畫在了  700 億到  900 億美
元之間，中位數  800 億。這是現在  82 億營收的整整⼗倍。
華爾街之前怎麼估的？⼤約  470 億。
「 800 億」這個⼤餅當然很吸引，但背後是由甚麼組成才是重要的。
我想和⼤家分享三個洞察。
洞察⼀：光通訊是「本命基本盤」，客製化只是「⾼槓桿期權」
在  Marvell 規劃的  2031 年  800 億美元營收藍圖中，我們可以把業務拆成兩⼤核
⼼：
( 其實還有交與儲存，通訊等等，但這些⽐較⼩，我們暫不討論 )

### Page 5

1. 互連與光通訊（ Interconnect ）： 預估營收  375 億美元。對⽐  2030 年該領
域  650 億美元的市場規模（ TAM ），這意味著  Marvell 預期⾃⼰將拿下⾼
達  58% 的市場份額。這是⼀種「準壟斷」地位。
2. 客製化晶⽚（ Custom Silicon ）： 預估營收  300 億美元。看起來數字也很
⼤，但對⽐  2030 年⾼達  2,350 億美元的客製化晶⽚市場， Marvell 的份額
其實只有  13% 。
雖然我在不同⽂章也說過很多次，但總是很多⼈仍然視 Marvell 為⼀家 ASIC 公
司。
但在客製化晶⽚市場裡， Marvell 依然只是⼀個挑戰  Broadcom 霸權的「挑戰
者」；但在光通訊與互連領域，它才是領導者。
什麼是互連？在⼀座⼤型  AI 資料中⼼裡⾯，擺滿了成千上萬顆  AI 晶⽚。

### Page 6

每⼀顆晶⽚就算運算速度再快，如果晶⽚跟晶⽚之間傳遞資料太慢，整座機房
就只能停下來空等。 Marvell 賣的產品，就是讓這些晶⽚能夠⾼速互相溝通的
關鍵零件。
這些零件包含了許多類型：例如「光訊號處理晶⽚（ DSP ）」，負責把電訊號轉
換成光訊號，再透過光纖傳送到另⼀個伺服器機櫃；「交換器晶⽚」，負責指揮
資料流量該往哪裡⾛；還有專⾨⽤來增強銅線訊號品質的晶⽚。
根據  Marvell 內部的預估，在伺服器機櫃內部、晶⽚與晶⽚之間互相傳輸的資
料，就佔了整座  AI 資料中⼼總流量的  85% 以上。
如果我們進⼀步把這兩組數據的「成⻑率」放在⼀起⽐對，會發現⼀個更值得
玩味的細節：
互連產品： Marvell 預估⾃家的營收年成⻑率約為  60% 到  70% ，⽽整個市
場的成⻑率⼤約也是  65% 。這意味著， Marvell 的預測前提並不是「我要
去搶下更多市場」，⽽是「我要在未來五年內，穩穩守住這過半的市占
率」。
客製化晶⽚： Marvell 預估⾃家的營收年成⻑率⾼達  75% 到  85% ，遠遠⾼

於整個市場  55% 的平均成⻑速度。這意味著， Marvell 的預測前提是「我
要發動猛烈進攻，從競爭對⼿的⼿中搶下更多的市占率」。
⼀守⼀攻之間，⾯臨的⾵險完全是兩回事。
在防守的這⼀邊（互連產品），公司最害怕的是「技術世代交替」。互連產品是
賣給所有客戶的標準化產品，不依賴單⼀買家。
但是，只要傳輸速度每提升⼀個世代（例如從  800G 到  1.6T ，再到  3.2T ），客
戶都會重新評估並選擇供應商。

### Page 7

每當技術進⼊世代交替的時候，原本穩固的市占率就有可能被競爭對⼿趁虛⽽
⼊。
在進攻的這⼀邊（客製化晶⽚），潛在客戶池本來就只有那幾家⼤型雲端服務
商。只要其中⼀家客戶決定把下⼀代產品的訂單轉給別⼈，或是決定把產品延
後⼀代推出，營收就會瞬間掉⼀⼤塊。
所以， Marvell 喊出的  800 億美元營收⽬標，是由兩個完全不同的賭注拼湊
⽽成的。
⼀個要求它在五年內絕對不能失守陣地，另⼀個則要求它在五年內必須持
續搶勝。這兩個條件必須同時成⽴，總⽬標才有可能順利兌現。
這⼀點在短期的業績調升中展現得淋漓盡致。
Marvell 將  2028 財年的營收⽬標從  180 億美元調升⾄  200 億美元，這多出來的
20 億美元，完全是由光通訊（ Scale-out/Scale-up optics ）和交換器晶⽚拉動
的，跟客製化晶⽚沒有關係。
正如執⾏⻑  Matt Murphy 所說：「是 Interconnect 在引領這場競賽。」
你買  Marvell ，買的其實是  AI 時代「數據傳輸」的物理剛需，客製化晶⽚

更多是附贈的超額期權。
還有⼀個現在幾乎是零、但可能改變遊戲的東⻄： scale-up 光學。
今天這塊的收⼊基本是零。管理層說放量是  2028 年的事。每顆  XPU 的光學元
件價值是數千美元，⽽且鏈路的兩端都需要光學。
Celestial AI 的光⼦互連技術，瞄準的是記憶體共享： HBM 距離處理器⼤約  1.5
毫⽶，所以今天加記憶體基本等於加處理器。
洞察⼆：⽑利率的障眼法，營業利益率才是真功夫
在展開之前，我們先把兩個名詞講清楚。因為接下來這段分析，全部建⽴在這
個區別上。
⽑利率  (Gross Margin)，是營收減去「直接製造成本」之後的⽐例。
所謂直接製造成本，就是晶圓、封裝、測試這些跟「做出這顆晶⽚」直接相關
的費⽤。它不包含研發、銷售、⾏政這些間接開銷。

### Page 8

營業利益率  (Operating Margin)，是把所有營運費⽤都扣完之後，真正剩下的
利潤⽐例。
除了直接製造成本，它還要扣掉研發費⽤、銷售費⽤、⾏政費⽤，這才是衡量
⼀家公司「到底賺不賺錢」的真實指標。
⼀句話總結：⽑利率告訴你「產品本⾝的利潤空間有多⼤」，營業利益率告訴
你「公司在扣完所有開銷之後，真正賺到⼿多少」。
現在，回到  Marvell 。
如果你有關注上⼀季的財報，你可能會記得：第三季的⽑利率指引從  58.9% 微
幅下滑到  57.5% 到  58.5% 。就這麼⼀點點，股價當天重挫了  10% 。
市場的解讀是，⽑利率降了，代表產品利潤在縮⽔，代表⽣意變差了，賣。
但這個結論並不是完全正確的。因為⽑利率下降的原因，不是產品利潤變薄
了，是「營收組合變了」。
這就要講到  Marvell 兩種產品的本質差異。
FOMO 研究院電⼦報  is a reader-supported
publication. To receive new posts and support

my work, consider becoming a free or paid
subscriber.
Subscribed
第⼀種：標準光通訊產品。⽑利率⾼，但研發費⽤重。
這類產品是  Marvell ⾃⼰設計、⾃⼰開發，然後賣給很多客戶。因為是標準化
產品，每顆晶⽚的直接製造成本相對低，所以⽑利率很⾼。
但代價是， Marvell 必須⾃⼰養⼀⼤批⼯程師，持續投⼊下⼀代產品的研發。
這些研發費⽤是  Marvell ⾃⼰承擔的，全部算在營業費⽤裡。
第⼆種：客製化晶⽚。⽑利率較低，但營業利益率⾮常好。
客製化晶⽚的直接製造成本⽐較⾼，因為要針對每個客戶的需求做定制化設計
和驗證，所以⽑利率看起來⽐較低。
但這裡有⼀個關鍵的結構差異：客製化專案的研發費⽤，⼤部分是由客戶承擔
的。業界叫  NRE （ Non-Recurring Engineering ），也就是客戶在專案啟動時就先
付⼀筆研發費。

### Page 9

這意味著， Marvell 做客製化晶⽚的時候，最貴的那塊「研發」，已經被客戶買
單了。
⼀旦進⼊量產，規模效應出來，每⼀顆晶⽚的邊際成本很低，不需要再額外投
⼊⼤量研發。
所以結果是：⽑利率⽐較低，但扣完所有費⽤之後，真正賺到⼿裡的利潤⽐例
反⽽更⾼。
現在你明⽩上次財報為什麼跌了。
當客製化晶⽚的營收佔⽐提⾼，整體⽑利率⾃然會往下⾛。因為客製化的⽑利
率⽐標準產品低。
但這不代表⽣意變差了。這代表營收正在從「⾼⽑利但研發⾃付」的產品，轉
向「低⽑利但研發由客戶買單」的產品。⽑利率表⾯上掉了，但營業利益率其
實在改善。
這次投資⼈⽇，管理層直接回答了這個問題。
他們沒有提⾼⽑利率的⻑期⽬標。它維持在  56% 到  59% 。意思是：⽑利率會
隨著產品組合波動，這是結構性的。
但他們把營業利益率的⻑期⽬標，從過去的  38% 到  40% ，⼀⼝氣拉到了  44%
到  46% 。整整多了六個百分點。
邏輯很簡單：當營收從  82 億成⻑到  800 億，研發團隊不會變成⼗倍⼤，銷售
團隊不會變成⼗倍⼤，⾏政費⽤不會變成⼗倍⼤。營業費⽤的增速，⼤約只有

營收增速的⼀半。

### Page 10

這就叫營運槓桿。規模越⼤，每⼀塊錢營收裡⾯，真正掉進股東⼝袋的⽐例就
越⾼。
管理層甚⾄給了⼀個對沖框架：
如果未來客製化晶⽚賣得⽐預期好，⽑利率會往  56% 那⼀端靠攏，但營業利益
率會被撐住，因為研發費是客戶出的。
如果光通訊賣得⽐預期好，⽑利率會往  59% 那⼀端⾛，但研發費⽤會⽐較重，
會吃掉⼀些。
不管哪⼀邊贏，營業利益率都應該守在  44% 到  46% 。
因此，最重要的數字，是營業利益率有沒有守住。
⽽那個「 30 in 30 」，也就是  2030 年  EPS 超過  30 美元，只有在這個營運槓桿真
的兌現的情況下才成⽴。
800 億的營收聽起來很厲害。但如果營業利益率守不住，那  800 億就只是營
收很⼤，結果⼤部分都拿去養⼯程師  and 付⾏政費⽤了，股東分不到多
少。
洞察三： Google 的  1200 億合約，暫時觀察就好
市場上另⼀個焦點，是媒體紛紛報導的「 Marvell 與  Google 簽署六年、總⾦額
1,200 億美元的龐⼤合作訂單」。

### Page 11

這個數字聽起來很嚇⼈。但如果你去翻今年  8 ⽉  18 ⽇  Marvell 提交給美國證管
會（ SEC ）的原始公告，會發現故事完全不是你想的那樣。
這份公告的真實內容是： Marvell 發放了⼀批「認股權證」給  Google 。這個權
利允許  Google 在未來，以每股  206.58 美元的約定價格，購買最多約  5,897 萬
股的  Marvell 股票。
但關鍵是，這批認股權證不是⼀次性給的。
除了第⼀年按季度發放的⼀⼩部分之外，其餘絕⼤部分被拆分成了  240 批。規
則很簡單： Google 每向  Marvell 採購滿  5 億美元的客製化產品，才可以解鎖其
中⼀批。
240 批，乘以每批  5 億美元，算出來的結果正好就是  1,200 億美元。
所以這個數字的真實含義是：如果  Google 想要拿滿所有的認股權證，它必須
先累計採購到  1,200 億美元。這是解鎖的「上限」，不是⼀張已經簽字畫押的採
購訂單。
換個⾓度想，這個合約結構其實透露了⼀個很有意思的事實：Marvell 需要拿

出⾃家公司的股票作為商業誘因，才能吸引  Google 在未來持續多下訂單。
⽽管理層在投資⼈⽇上的說法也⾮常誠實。他們表⽰， 2031 年的財務預測中，
只納⼊了這筆合作的「⼀⼩部分」。⽽且他們強調：「即便未來沒有拿到任何新
的設計案，也能穩穩落在  700 到  900 億美元的預測區間內。」
這句話的意思是：那  1,200 億的天花板，根本不在  2031 年的預測模型裡。它是
⼀個更⻑期的可能性，不是眼前的保證。
不過，這裡有⼀個細節必須釐清。
管理層在投資⼈⽇上強調的是「沒有任何單⼀  socket 超過營收的  10% 」，⽽不
是「沒有任何單⼀客戶」。
Socket 是⼀顆晶⽚在客戶系統裡佔的那個位⼦（設計席位）。同⼀個客戶可以
有很多個  socket 。⽐如  Amazon ，它的下⼀代  Trainium 、光  DSP 、交換器、機
房間互連，加起來就是四個不同的  socket 。
所以，「單⼀  socket 不超過  10% 」，只代表沒有「單獨⼀顆晶⽚」⼤到能決定全

年營收。這並不意味著客戶集中度降低了， Amazon 依然是  Marvell 最⼤的客
戶。
Marvell 真正在做的，是「在同⼀個⼤客戶裡，塞進更多不同種類的晶⽚」。他
們現在已經同時向美國四⼤超⼤規模數據中⼼出貨客製化晶⽚，並且在每個客

### Page 12

戶內部贏下了多個設計席位。
客製化業務已經從「靠單⼀產品押寶」，⾛向了「⽤產品矩陣深度綁定」。這才
是它作為期權最真實的狀態。

真正的信號：⼗個⽉，四次上調
不過⽼實說，現在距離 2031 年還有 5 年。在 AI 時代， 5 個⽉也嫌太多。任何遠期
預測，往往只是基於「 AI 資本⽀出會無限膨脹」的樂觀外推。
所以 2031 年的預測數字，聽聽就好。
真正能驗證  Marvell 底氣的，不是  2031 年的  800 億，⽽是過去⼗個⽉內，
管理階層將  2028 年的營收⽬標連續調升了四次（從  130 億、 150 億、 180
億，⼀路拔⾼到現在的  200 億）。
這種短期的、⾼頻率的預期上修，是無法靠吹⽜粉飾的。它背後代表的是實打

實、正在出貨的光模組與交換器晶⽚。
Marvell 賣的不是「另⼀家定制  ASIC 廠商」的故事。

### Page 13

它賣的是：是⼀個互聯的霸主，加上⼀張在定制市場裡搶份額的期權，再加上
⼀個因為規模效應⽽重新定價的利潤率模型。
「 Marvell 2031 年要做到  800 億」，這句話的解讀應該是：那  800 億裡⾯，多少
是你已經看⾒的，多少是你必須相信的？
答案的⽐例，決定了你願意付多少倍。
Marvell 應該如何解讀，我早在半年前， Marvell 還沒爆升的時候和⼤家
深⼊分析過，裡⾯的邏輯仍然成⽴，有興趣的朋友不妨重溫分析。
雲端巨頭為何急需 ASIC ？博通為何估值
⽐ NVDA 還⾼？光通訊是 Marvell 重要
⽀柱？ - 深⼊分析第 38 期：
Broadcom+Marvell
KP@FOMOSOC · MAR 11
想像⼀下，你正坐在 Google 或 Meta 的基礎設施決策會議裡。桌上擺著今年剛被
董事會批准的龐⼤資本⽀出（ CapEx ）預算：⼀個⾼達千多億美元的鉅額數
字。
Read full story

### Page 14

主題⼆  | Google 簽約
Constellation Energy ，核電準備
起⾶？
當  Google 宣布砸下  43 億美元跟  Constellation Energy 合作時，你可能第⼀個反
應是：核電公司終於要起⾶了嗎？
但事實上， Google 這次砸下重⾦，是去讓 Constellation Energy 升級  11 座現有的
核電機組，預計在  2028 到  2032 年間「憑空擠出」 890 MW 的發電容量。
這在產業裡有個專有名詞，叫做「核電升級擴容（ Nuclear Uprates ）」。
如果把能源產業⽐喻成房地產，現在市場上最值錢的，早就不是⼀塊什麼都沒
有、需要重新整地、環評、等上  15 年才能蓋好的荒地。
現在最值錢的，是⼀棟：⼟地早就買好、建照早就拿到、⽔電管線全部接
妥、裡⾯甚⾄已經住滿房客，⽽且頂樓「居然還能合法再加蓋三層樓」的
現成⼤樓。
這多出來的「三層樓」，就是  AI 巨頭們現在不惜代價、⽤  20 年⻑約也要搶下
來的⿈⾦資產。

時間，是  AI 擴張最⼤的敵⼈
為什麼  Google 選擇升級舊廠，⽽⾮蓋新廠？因為蓋新核電廠最⼤的敵⼈從來
不是技術，⽽是時間。

### Page 15

美國喬治亞州的  Vogtle 核電廠新增兩個反應爐，從動⼯到商轉整整花了⼗五
年，建造成本更是天⽂數字。
相⽐之下，我們來算⼀筆帳： Google 這次幫  CEG 出資  43 億美元換取  890 MW
的新增容量，折算下來，每千⽡（ kW ）的建置成本⼤約是  4,800 美元。
這個數字，僅僅是  Vogtle 新建機組單位成本的幾分之⼀，⽽且速度快了近⼗
年。
「升級擴容」這種特定的增量⽅式，既便宜，⼜剛好能趕上  2028 到  2032 年這
段最致命的缺電窗⼝。
缺電有多急迫？負責管理美國多州電網的  PJM 在最新報告中預估， 2028 到
2029 年就會出現⼤約  6.8 吉⽡（ GW ）的可靠電⼒缺⼝，⽽資料中⼼正是主要
來源。
但在  PJM 最近⼀輪的容量拍賣中，全新機組加上擴容項⽬，最終竟然只成功出
清了約  525 MW 。
⾯對  6.8 GW 的無底深淵，這點新增電⼒根本是杯⽔⾞薪。資料中⼼的擴張速
度，不可能乖乖等上⼗五年。
過去，核電業⾯臨最⼤的難題是前期投資太龐⼤。

如果你要砸  40 億美元升級設備，董事會⼀定會問：未來⼆⼗年的電價怎麼
算？如果五年後電價⼤跌，這筆錢就⽩花了。
現在， Google 和  Amazon 站出來了。他們直接簽下⼗五到⼆⼗年的⻑期購電合
約，把未來的訂單提前包下來。
就在  Google 宣布這筆交易的前⼀週， Amazon 才剛跟  Constellation Energy 達成
另⼀筆包含  190 百萬⽡升級項⽬的⻑約。兩家巨頭加起來，實質⽀持了超過  1
吉⽡的核電增量。
這等同於科技巨頭⽤⾃⾝的信⽤，幫核電業者⼤幅降低了投資⾵險。原本在試
算表上內部報酬率不夠漂亮、不值得投資的升級專案，瞬間變成了華爾街最愛
的穩定獲利資產。
AI 不僅帶來了電⼒需求，更徹底改變了核電產業的融資⽅式。
看懂「升級潛⼒」這個新指標
如果這個邏輯成⽴，⾦融市場對核電公司的估值⽅式也要跟著改寫。

### Page 16

以前評估⼀家核電營運商，⼤家看的是總發電量、電價和燃料成本。但未來，
有⼀個更重要的指標叫做「升級擴容潛⼒」。
市場會開始問：你⼿上的反應爐還可以再增加多少發電量？每增加⼀度電需要
多少成本？美國核管會核准需要多久？你能不能找到像  Google 這種⼤客戶簽
⻑約？
假設有兩家公司，各⾃擁有  10 吉⽡的核電容量。 A 公司的機組已經操到技術
極限，沒有升級餘地。
B 公司的機組還能再擴容  800 百萬⽡，⽽且執照能⼀路延⻑到  2050 年。在現
在的市場眼中，這兩家公司的經濟價值絕對是天壤之別。

反向銷售 Gemini
這筆交易另⼀個有趣的點在於：Google 同時是客戶，也是技術供應商。
在簽下購電協議的同時， CEG 也與  Google 簽署了為期  5 年的技術合作，導⼊

Google Cloud 與  Gemini Enterprise 。⽽  Gemini 會在三個領域使⽤：
加速電⼒開發、選址與併網規劃。
優化核電設備的健康監測與停機維護。
保護電網與核電設施的關鍵營運系統。
Google 拿著⼤筆資⾦去買電，轉過⾝來，⼜把⾃⼰的  AI 與雲端服務賣進了能
源產業最核⼼、最敏感、也最不容出錯的營運系統裡。
⼀旦  Gemini 成功在  CEG 的  11 座核電機組中證明了⾃⼰能優化停機時間、預
測設備故障，這套系統將成為  Google Cloud 攻佔全球公⽤事業市場最強⼤的活
廣告。
升級不是無限魔法

### Page 17

不過，核電升級擴容是有天花板的。你不可能把⼀座  1 吉⽡的反應爐，⼀路改
造成  2 吉⽡。
美國核管會將升級分為不同類型，規模最⼤的擴展功率升級，最多也只能增加
約  20% 的發電量，⽽且需要⼤規模設備改造。
這意味著，當市場上那些最容易升級的反應爐都被榨乾之後，下⼀個需要的百
萬⽡電⼒，最終還是得靠蓋新核電廠、天然氣，或是再⽣能源搭配儲能來補
⾜。
所以，在觀察核電產業時，我們可以把公司分成兩種。⼀種是只靠著核能題材
在說故事的公司，另⼀種是⼿上真的握有可以變現、且還有升級空間的反應爐
的實⼒派。
這場由  AI 驅動的核能復興確實正在發⽣。
但最直接的贏家，是那些在⼆、三⼗年前蓋好、以前⼤家覺得無聊透頂，
現在卻能替  AI 時代擠出關鍵電⼒的舊核電廠。
我之前寫過兩篇電⼒的分析，分別分析了公⽤電⼒發電商，以及獨⽴
發電商的邏輯。⽽在獨⽴發電商中，⼜再分成不同的種類。有興趣的
朋友不妨重溫分析。
核能復興應該買甚麼？獨⽴發電商如何分
析？ - 深⼊分析第 42 期： CEG ， VST ，

TLN ， OKLO ( 電⼒系列下集 )
KP@FOMOSOC · APR 8

### Page 18

2024 年  9 ⽉，三哩島（ Three Mile Island ）核電廠， Constellation Energy 的執⾏
⻑ Joe Dominguez 站在這個曾經因為  1979 年美國史上最嚴重核災⽽舉世聞名、
甚⾄帶有幾分禁忌⾊彩的地⽅，向全世界宣布了⼀項重要的決定：
Read full story
主題三： SpaceX 準備下場做電信
商？
美國⼈這幾⼗年來⽤的⼿機⾨號服務，基本上都離不開三家⼤公司： AT&T 、
Verizon 和  T-Mobile 。
這三巨頭花了幾⼗年的時間來建設⾏動網路，砸⼤錢買下⼤量的無線電頻譜、
蓋基地台、鋪設光纖網路，築起了⼀道外⼈很難跨越的超⾼競爭⾨檻。
不過，兩天前， SpaceX 和  Grain Management 達成協議，準備買下對⽅⼿中的
⼀批  800 MHz 低頻頻譜。
這批頻譜最寬的郡⼤約有  14 MHz 的成對頻段，部分地區甚⾄只有  5 MHz 左
右。整筆交易還需要經過  FCC 核准，⽬前尚未完成交割。

根據媒體報導，這筆交易⼤約花了  80 億美元。消息⼀出，這三⼤電信巨頭的
股價在盤後全⾯⼤跌，跌幅達到  6% 到  7% 。
為什麼只是買下⼀段看不⾒的無線電波頻率，就會讓這些傳統電信商的市值瞬
間⼤縮⽔？ SpaceX ⼜在準備甚麼策略？

### Page 19

要搞懂這件事，我們得先了解  SpaceX 過去到底還缺了什麼武器，以及它未來
打算怎麼打這場仗。
從「補死⾓」到「搶客戶」
⼤家都聽過的星鏈（ Starlink ），是靠著低軌道衛星來提供網路服務。
⼀開始，你想⽤星鏈的網路，還得在⾃家裝個專⾨的接收盤。後來  SpaceX 推
出了「 Direct-to-Cell 」技術，讓⼀般的智慧型⼿機直接連上衛星，不⽤再另外
花錢買傳統那種笨重的衛星電話。
這聽起來超厲害，但它⼀開始的商業⽤途，主要是為了補救傳統基地台訊號打
不到的地⽅。
舉例來說，當你開⾞經過荒郊野外的公路，附近半個基地台都沒有的時候，⼿
機還是可以靠天上衛星傳求救訊息或是簡訊。
所以，早期  SpaceX 其實是跟電信商  T-Mobile 合作的。 T-Mobile 繼續賺客戶的
⽉租費，⽽  SpaceX 則是幫忙填補網路訊號的死⾓。
這原本是⼀個雙贏的安排，⼤家各取所需。
但問題是，死⾓批發和⾃⼰賣⾨號，是兩種完全不同的⽣意。
前者賺的是供應商的錢。⽤戶每⽉付給  T-Mobile ， SpaceX 只是在基地台打不到

的地⽅把簡訊和少量數據接上衛星。
客戶名單、品牌、⽉租定價、加價賣⽅案的權⼒，通通都在  T-Mobile ⼿上。衛
星⽤得越多，越像⼀項被包進電信商資費裡的功能，越不像⼀⾨獨⽴⽣意。
T-Mobile 的執⾏⻑甚⾄公開說過，衛星覆蓋以後會變成標配，不再是差異化。
⾔下之意就是，配⾓的批發費，遲早會隨著這件事變便宜。
後者賺的才是電信商的錢。⾨號⽣意的核⼼在於你擁有客戶、擁有帳單、擁有
定價權。
問題來了， SpaceX 現在已經不⽢於只當個配⾓。
今年  8 ⽉， SpaceX 的總裁  Gwynne Shotwell 很明⽩地表⽰，公司有意⾃⼰蓋地
⾯網路，把美國無線市場估在⼤約  6,000 億美元，直接跟⼤型電信商正⾯對
決。

### Page 20

到了  10 ⽉， SpaceX 更在官⽅公告裡放話，Starlink Mobile 的最終⽬標，就是
要成為美國主要的⼿機電信業者。
這代表  SpaceX 想做的，早就超出了「讓你在深⼭裡能收到⼀格訊號」的範
圍。
它是希望有⼀天，你可以直接打電話退掉  Verizon 的⽉租⽅案，改向  Starlink
Mobile 繳錢辦⼿機⾨號。
當「合作夥伴」公開宣⽰要「搶客戶」的時候，雙⽅的關係就產⽣了質
變。
電信商的防禦同盟
要理解這場博弈，我們得先聊聊電信業的⼀個特殊⾓⾊：MVNO （虛擬⾏動網
路電信商）。
MVNO 就是那些⾃⼰沒有實體基地台，靠租⽤  AT&T 、 Verizon 或  T-Mobile 的
網路來賣⾨號的公司，⽐如美國的  Mint Mobile 或  Google Fi 。
對新進者來說，當  MVNO 是最省錢、最快的捷徑。
對任何想進⼊電信市場的玩家⽽⾔，最省錢的劇本都是先跟電信商批發地⾯網
路，在城市和室內使⽤現成的訊號，再搭配⾃⼰的技術補強死⾓。
這樣⼀來，就能⽤極輕的資產，迅速推出⼀張「全美無死⾓」的資費⽅案。

但這個模式能順利推進的前提，是「互補⼤於競爭」。當  SpaceX 只是幫忙補死
⾓時，電信商很樂意賺這筆批發費。
可是，當  SpaceX 公開宣⽰要搶客戶、甚⾄準備⾃⼰買頻譜蓋地⾯網路時，三
⼤巨頭聯⼿警覺：把城市核⼼網路租給⼀個準備⽤低價策略殺價的對⼿，等於
是在割⾃⼰的⾁。批發賺來的錢，根本彌補不了被挖⾛的⽉租費與定價權。
這條原本可能存在的合作路徑，正在迅速變窄。
電信商的防禦動作極其迅速。 10 ⽉  1 ⽇， AT&T 、 T-Mobile 和  Verizon 正式
簽署合約成⽴合資公司，將部分頻譜集中起來，⽤來補⾜傳統基地台打不
到的死⾓。
合資聲明寫得很溫和，但華爾街普遍將此解讀為對  Starlink Mobile 的戰略防
禦。

### Page 21

這場防禦的訊號，在細節中展露無遺。 T-Mobile 悄悄將官網⾸⾴上的「 with
Starlink 」字樣撤下，在⾨⾯上與  SpaceX 劃清界線。
三⼤電信商試圖⾃⼰掌控頻譜主導權，組建⼀個不以  Starlink 為核⼼夥伴的死
⾓平台。
⽽當三⼤電信商組成同盟時，他們需要⼀個衛星通訊的同盟，⽽這個很可
能會是  AST SpaceMobile 。

SpaceX 的破局：買下  800 MHz ⾃⼰建網
既然租電信商城市網路的難度越來越⾼， SpaceX 決定⾃⼰買頻譜。
我們可以把無線頻譜想像成⼀條⾼速公路。⾺路越寬，同⼀時間能塞進去的⾞
⼦通常就越多；可是，如果這條路根本開不到你家⾨⼝，那⾞⼦再多也沒⽤。

通訊頻譜有兩個最關鍵的問題：第⼀是訊號能傳多遠，第⼆是能同時塞多少上
網資料。
( 關於頻譜的詳細分析，可以回看我之前講衛星的深⼊分析，在這裡不再詳述 )
SpaceX 之前透過  EchoStar 拿到了⼀段  2 GHz 的頻譜，這可以當作衛星通訊服
務很重要的「容量」來源。但是，頻率⽐較⾼的訊號有⼀個缺點，遇到牆壁之
類的障礙物⽐較容易被擋住。
⽽這次買下的  800 MHz 頻率⽐較低、波⻑⽐較⻑，不僅傳播條件好，穿透部分
建築物的能⼒也⽐較有優勢。
簡單來說， 2 GHz 負責提供傳輸⼤量資料的空間， 800 MHz 負責鋪設⼀張無死
⾓的覆蓋網。
這讓  SpaceX 拿到了⾃⼰蓋「地⾯網路」的⼊場券，可以把天上的第⼆代衛
星、地⾯的⼩型基地台以及這張低頻訊號網結合起來，打造⼀個完全屬於
⾃⼰的「 Starlink Mobile 」。

### Page 22

有趣的是，這批  800 MHz 頻譜的源頭，其實是  T-Mobile 在  2020 年併購  Sprint
時被政府要求剝離的資產。
T-Mobile ⾃⼰建⽴全國  5G 覆蓋網⽤的是  600 MHz 頻段， 800 MHz 對它⽽⾔只
是重複的冗餘覆蓋。把⼀個⾃⼰根本沒在⽤的資產賣掉，換回  29 億美元現⾦
加上⾃⼰真正需要的  600 MHz 頻譜，這是⼀筆極度理性的資產置換。
後來這批資產轉到了  Grain ⼿中，如今⼜被  SpaceX 以近  80 億美元的⾼價接
⼿。
對  SpaceX ⽽⾔，全國性的低頻頻譜幾乎不可能從三⼤巨頭⼿中流出，這段
800 MHz ，是⾺斯克在美國建⽴「獨⽴⼿機網路」難得能買到的⾨票。
⾃建網的物理現實與時間賽跑
就算拿到了頻譜， SpaceX 接下來⾯對的依然是極度現實的物理限制。
800 MHz 頻譜確實能改善覆蓋範圍，但它無法憑空創造無限的網路容量。
這次買下的頻寬最厚只有  14 MHz ，在某些地區甚⾄只有  5 MHz 。

這是⼀層極薄的覆蓋毯。如果幾⼗萬⼈同時擠在紐約曼哈頓市中⼼看影⽚、開
視訊會議，這種低頻薄頻寬根本撐不住。
城市尖峰時刻的龐⼤資料流量，依然需要⾼頻頻譜與密集的基地台。
因此， SpaceX 的建網策略注定是「衛星為主，微型基站為輔」。
它可能會把⼩型基站直接掛在⽤戶家中的  Starlink 接收盤旁邊，或者只在有⾼
密度需求的地⽅按需部署。這種做法資本⽀出極低，覆蓋範圍會跟著⽤戶慢慢
⻑出來。
這意味著  SpaceX 開台第⼀天，絕對無法提供全國室內無死⾓的完美體驗。
真正的護城河：不在地⾯，在⽕箭
很多⼈盯著  800 MHz 的薄頻寬，覺得這不⾜以撼動三⼤巨頭，卻忽略了
SpaceX 真正的殺⼿鐧：發射成本與星座規模。
SpaceX ⾃⼰造衛星、⾃⼰⽤⽕箭打上去， FCC 最近還批准了多達  15,000 顆下
⼀代  Direct-to-Cell 衛星的部署。

### Page 23

這種「太空基礎設施」的規模優勢，是  AT&T 或  Verizon 花多少錢都買不到
的，也是其他衛星新創難以企及的。
當傳統電信商在地⾯為了幾  MHz 的頻譜⽄⽄計較、為了基地台選址和地⽅政
府博弈時， SpaceX 正在頭頂上佈建⼀張不受地形限制的⽴體網路。
天上⽤下⼀代衛星直連⼿機提供廣覆蓋，地⾯⽤剛買下的  800 MHz 鋪⼀層
穿透⼒強的基礎網。
這套組合拳，才是  SpaceX 敢於直接賣⾨號的底氣。
定價權的倒數計時
根據公司近期的說法，⽐較有意義的  5G 級衛星直連服務，⼤概要到  2028 年上
半年才會開始上線。
即使屆時正式開台，初期體驗也不會完美。室外死⾓會明顯減少，對價格敏
感、不在乎極致網速的⽤戶有吸引⼒；但城市室內與尖峰時段的容量，仍會明
顯落後三⼤電信商。
SpaceX 開台第⼀天賣的，會是⼀個「覆蓋更廣、價格可能更低的基本服務」，
⽽不是⽴刻全⾯取代現有的⾼速體驗。
但是， SpaceX 不需要提供完美的  5G 體驗，只要它提供⼀個「⾜夠便宜且沒有

死⾓」的替代品，就⾜以讓那些對價格敏感的消費者⼼動，進⽽打破電信巨頭
們多年來維持的價格默契。
從投資的⾓度來看，市場重新定價的焦點，早就超越了「明年會流失多少⽤
戶」。
真正被放上天平的，是未來⼗年內，這些依賴⾼資本⽀出築起護城河的⽼字號
公司，是否還能維持過去那種穩定且豐厚的利潤率。
當破壞者拿到了低頻頻譜這張⼊場券，並且擁有對⼿無法複製的太空優勢時，
定價權的流失，往往會⽐⽤戶的流失來得更快。
關於太空，我在年初的時候寫了兩篇，分為衛星和發射的兩篇，有興
趣的朋友可以重溫當中邏輯。

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
TODAY’S INSIGHTS
Financial Markets
 Markets regained some footing to close off the week after 
President Trump said the US would not strike Iran before 
next month’s midterm elections, supporting risk sentiment 
and helping equities recover. However, tensions flared again 
over the weekend, with an attack on Saudi Arabia’s main 
airport and a US strike on a commercial tanker in the Gulf of 
Oman. On Friday, President Trump also announced that Russia 
would supply 13.5 million barrels of diesel to global markets 
through October to help ease fuel shortages and lower prices, 
a move that would mark a significant departure from years 
of sanctions despite the ongoing war in Ukraine. US bond 
markets will be closed on Monday for Columbus Day, although 
equity markets remain open. 
• US equities closed higher on Friday, capping a volatile week 
that saw sharp moves in both bond yields and energy markets. 
The S&P 500 rose 0.6%, although it remained below the 
record high reached earlier in the week. The Dow Jones gained 
0.8%, while the Nasdaq also advanced 0.8%, rebounding 
from Thursday’s tech-led sell-off amid concerns over the pace 
of AI-related growth. European equities outperformed as 
volatility in regional bond markets eased, with the Euro Stoxx 
50 rising 0.8% and the FTSE 100 up 1.1%.
• Asian equities delivered a mixed performance on Friday. The 
Nikkei 225 closed flat, recovering from an intraday decline of 
1.3% after reports that OpenAI’s revenue targets may indeed 
be reached. The CSI 300 was up 0.2%, while the Hang Seng 
rose 1.8%, supported by gains in technology stocks. The ASX 
200 advanced 0.6%, with futures pointing to a further gain at 
today’s open. South Korea’s Kospi was closed for Hangul Day.
• US Treasury yields were little changed as investors digested 
President Trump’s pledge not to take military action against 
Iran until after the midterm elections. The 2-year and 10-
year Treasury rose 3bps and 1bp respectively, with the latter 
holding at 5.24%, below the 24-year high reached earlier in 
the week. Attention now turns to the September CPI report, 
due Wednesday night (11:30pm AEDT), and comments from 
Fed Chair Warsh, both of which are expected to shape market 
expectations ahead of the FOMC’s October meeting. Futures 
currently assign a 17% probability to an October rate hike and 
have almost fully priced in a hike by December, leaving the 
implied year-end policy rate at 4.13%. 
•  European bonds ended lower after a volatile week. The 
10-year bund yield fell 1bp, while the 10-year gilts declined 
4bps. France’s 10-year OAT yield also dropped 4bps, although 
concerns over the government’s ability to reduce the budget 
deficit to 5% of GDP by 2027 continue to weigh on sentiment.
• In Australia, the 3-year government bond yield was 
unchanged at 4.90%, while the 10-year yield fell 6bps to 
5.34%. Attention this week will centre on the September 
Labour Force Survey, due Thursday, where a moderation in 
the participation rate from its near 115-year high is expected 
to keep the unemployment rate broadly steady (see our 
preview). Swap markets continue to assign only a modest 
probability to further policy tightening, with around 26% 
odds of a November rate hike currently priced.
• The US dollar index rose modestly, gaining 0.1%. The AUD 
rose against the greenback, up 0.3% to 0.6978. Other crosses 
against the USD were mixed, with the yen up 0.2%, euro 
losing 0.2% and the pound flat.
• Commodities were mostly higher. Oil prices recovered from 
early losses, with Brent crude rising 0.4% to US$104.7/bbl 
and WTI also gaining 0.4%. Elsewhere, gold advanced 1.5% 
and copper rose 1.6%, while iron ore bucked the trend, falling 
0.7% amid a subdued outlook for demand. 
International Data
In the US, the University of Michigan consumer sentiment 
deteriorated marginally in the preliminary October print, the 
headline index declining from 48.1 to 46.3. Current conditions 
fell from 50.9 to 44.7 while expectations edged up to 47.3. 
Historically low sentiment is yet to stop consumers from 
spending though, with real consumption growth remaining 
robust through 2026.

Today’s key data and events

- CN M2 Money Supply Sep 7.4% 7.5%

consensus forecasts.

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 điểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 điểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao 
phục vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức 
cao cho phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện 
đáng kể trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm 
phán vẫn đang bế tắc và ít có khả năng dòng chảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối 
lại ngay trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi lựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đầu tư công, chính sách tiền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

I. Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
TTCK Việt Nam đi ngang, giảm nhẹ 
trong 9 tháng đầu năm 
 TTCK Việt Nam biến động giằng co, giảm điểm nhẹ trong 9 tháng đầu năm với 
diễn biến có thể chia làm 2 giai đoạn chính. Giai đoạn 1 kéo dài đến gần cuối 
quý 1, thị trường đi ngang trong 2 tháng đầu, trước khi lao dốc mạnh từ cuối 
tháng 2 trước ảnh hưởng bởi xung đột tại Trung Đông bùng phát. Giai đoạn 2 là 
khoảng thời gian còn lại, thị trường xuất hiện các nhịp biến động mạnh, 
tăng/giảm đan xen, chịu ảnh hưởng bởi thông tin về giá dầu, biến động lãi suất 
trong nước, kỳ vọng về nâng hạng thị trường và các yếu tố cơ bản khác. Tính 
chung 9 tháng, VNIndex gần như đi ngang, giảm nhẹ 0.9% so với cuối 2025, 
trong khi thanh khoản bình quân toàn thị trường giảm xấp xỉ 12% so với cùng 
kỳ.

### Page 4

Với diễn biến căng thẳng tại Trung Đông vẫn đang dai dẳng, đồng thời dự báo 
mặt bằng lãi suất sẽ khó xuất hiện xu hướng hạ nhiệt rõ nét trong năm 2026, dù 
vẫn có những điểm sáng vĩ mô và KQKD khả quan của doanh nghiệp, các dự 
báo của chúng tôi nhìn chung có phần thận trọng hơn so với dự báo gần nhất. 
Cụ thể:

Lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm 
yết dự báo tăng trưởng mạnh

Chúng tôi điều chỉnh dự báo tăng trưởng EPS toàn thị trường cho năm 2026 lên 
mức 33% YoY, chủ yếu nhờ phản ánh mức tăng 200% kế hoạch lợi nhuận sau 
thuế của VIC (loại trừ mức tăng trưởng đột biến này của VIC, tăng trưởng EPS 
toàn thị trường ở mức 17% YoY). Đóng góp chính đến từ mức tăng của một số 
nhóm có tỷ trọng lớn: Bất động sản (+79% YoY), Nguyên vật liệu (+44% YoY) và 
Hàng tiêu dùng thiết yếu (+27% YoY). Mức tăng trưởng EPS cao, tương đồng với 
số liệu tăng trưởng kinh tế vĩ mô tích cực, là động lực quan trọng hỗ trợ thị 
trường trong trung/dài hạn.

Bối cảnh vĩ mô quốc tế còn nhiều 
bất định

Bối cảnh kinh tế toàn cầu trong giai đoạn cuối năm vẫn chịu áp lực lớn từ xung 
đột địa chính trị ở Trung Đông và động thái điều hành chính sách của Fed. Đàm 
phán Mỹ – Iran chưa đạt tiến triển đáng kể, trong khi giao tranh tiếp diễn và 
hoạt động vận chuyển dầu bị gián đoạn, làm gia tăng rủi ro thiếu hụt nguồn 
cung và khiến giá dầu được dự báo tiếp tục neo cao trong thời gian tới. Giá 
năng lượng duy trì ở mức cao, cùng với áp lực lạm phát chưa hạ nhiệt rõ rệt, 
khiến Fed tiếp tục duy trì lập trường thận trọng và không loại trừ khả năng tiếp 
tục thắt chặt chính sách tiền tệ trong quý 4.

Mặt bằng lãi suất huy động dự báo 
duy trì mức cao trong quý 4

Chúng tôi duy trì đánh giá tích cực về triển vọng tăng trưởng kinh tế Việt Nam 
năm 2026, với GDP dự kiến tăng 8.5–9.0%, dựa trên kỳ vọng cán cân thương 
mại cải thiện, dòng vốn FDI tiếp tục hỗ trợ tăng trưởng và đầu tư công được đẩy 
mạnh. Tuy nhiên, giá năng lượng là rủi ro đáng chú ý, có thể gây thêm áp lực 
lên cán cân thương mại và lạm phát, qua đó ảnh hưởng đến triển vọng tăng 
trưởng trong thời gian tới. 
Áp lực thiếu hụt thanh khoản ngắn hạn đã có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt, trong khi áp 
lực huy động vốn trung và dài hạn vẫn hiện hữu khi nhu cầu vốn của nền kinh 
tế dự kiến ở mức cao. Do đó, nhìn về cuối năm, chúng tôi cho rằng lãi suất chưa 
thể xuất hiện xu hướng giảm rõ rệt, nhưng kỳ vọng có thể ổn định quanh mặt 
bằng hiện tại nhờ hoạt động giải ngân đầu tư công tiếp tục hỗ trợ dòng tiền và 
NHNN vẫn còn dư địa điều tiết thanh khoản trong bối cảnh áp lực tỷ giá không 
lớn và áp lực lạm phát vẫn trong tầm kiểm soát.

Biểu đồ 5. P/E forward 2026 các thị trường  Biểu đồ 6. Biến động EPS nhóm ASEAN4

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV  Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV

Định giá chỉ số VNIndex đang ở 
vùng hấp dẫn 
 P/E của chỉ số VNIndex hiện đang ở mức 13.5 (theo Bloomberg), thấp hơn đáng 
kể so với mức bình quân 5 năm gần nhất ở 15.1.  
Nếu loại trừ mức tăng đột biến ở nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup từ đầu năm 2025 đến 
nay, P/E của thị trường (sau đây gọi là P/E điều chỉnh) chỉ còn ở mức 10.9 lần. Đây 
là mức thấp hơn cả các đáy gần nhất trong quá khứ như đáy Covid vào tháng 
3/2020 (ở 11.3 lần), đáy của năm 2022 (ở 11.01 lần), và đáy thuế quan trong 
năm 2025 (ở 12 lần).

Lưu ý: chúng tôi tính toán P/E điều chỉnh với giả định P/E nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup 
giữ nguyên từ đầu năm 2025 (hay nói cách khác, biến động giá ở nhóm cổ 
phiếu này tương đồng với lợi nhuận trượt 4 quý). Cách tính này giúp loại bỏ ảnh 
hưởng của nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup lên VNIndex, khi mà riêng 2 cổ phiếu VIC, 
VHM đã đóng góp xấp xỉ 450 điểm cho mức tăng của chỉ số VNIndex từ đầu năm 
2025, và P/E của VIC hiện đang giao dịch ở mức 80 lần.

Chỉ số VNIndex được dự báo ở 
quanh mức 1,800 điểm thời điểm 
cuối năm 2026 
 Đối với triển vọng thị trường trong quý cuối năm 2026, với đánh giá mặt bằng 
lãi suất sẽ tiếp tục duy trì ở mức cao, kết hợp với xu hướng tăng trưởng lợi 
nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết vẫn sẽ được duy trì, chúng tôi dự báo vùng 
điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 1,800 điểm, tương 
đương mức tăng gần 2% so với đóng cửa quý 3. Dự báo này tương ứng:

- Mức tăng trưởng EPS 2026 của thị trường chung được kỳ vọng tăng 
mạnh lên mức 33% (tăng so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ 
yếu nhờ kỳ vọng vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và 
lợi nhuận tăng trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt 
động kinh doanh).

- Về mặt định giá, chúng tôi giảm nhẹ vùng P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIn-
dex thời điểm cuối năm xuống 13.2 (từ mức 13.5 ở thời điểm hiện tại - 
phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là vùng 
định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm, và vùng thấp lịch sử nếu loại bỏ 
đà tăng đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup từ đầu năm 2025 (thể hiện ở chỉ số 
P/E điều chỉnh mà chúng tôi nhận định ở trên). Đây là cơ sở để chúng tôi 
kỳ vọng thị trường sẽ có nhịp bật tăng mạnh ngay khi môi trường vĩ mô 
quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran) và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn đề lãi 
suất) chuyển biến theo chiều hướng tích cực. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi cho 
rằng các cải thiện này sẽ cần chờ đến nửa đầu 2027, vì vậy chúng tôi 
chưa nâng mức P/E hợp lý của thị trường ngay trong báo cáo này.
